      overflowY: dimensions().height < targetHeight ? "auto" : naturalOverflowY });
    const place = () => {
      if (motionTone !== "menu" || disposed) return;
      const base = node.getBoundingClientRect(), rect = trigger?.getBoundingClientRect();
      const width = surface.offsetWidth, height = surface.offsetHeight;
      let left = base.left, top = base.top;
      if (rect) {
        if (side === "top" || side === "bottom") {
          left = align === "end" ? rect.right - width : align === "center" ? rect.left + (rect.width - width) / 2 : rect.left;
          top = side === "top" ? rect.bottom - height : rect.top;
        } else {
          // Navigation and cascading menus keep their original side placement.
          left = base.left + (side === "left" ? node.offsetWidth - width : 0);
          top = base.top;
        }
        left = Math.max(8, Math.min(left, window.innerWidth - width - 8));
        top = Math.max(8, Math.min(top, window.innerHeight - height - 8));
        compact.style.left = `${rect.left - left - borderLeft}px`;
        compact.style.top = `${rect.top - top - borderTop}px`;
      }
      surface.style.left = `${left - base.left - borderLeft}px`;
      surface.style.top = `${top - base.top - borderTop}px`;
    };
    const isOpen = () => node.getAttribute("data-state") === "open" || node.hasAttribute("data-open");
    let faceReady = false, pointerHeld = false;
    let releaseFrame = 0;
    const enablePointer = () => {
      if (motionTone === "menu") body.style.pointerEvents = isOpen() && faceReady && !pointerHeld ? "auto" : "none";
    };
    const releasePointer = () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(releaseFrame);
      releaseFrame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        pointerHeld = false;
        if (latest.current.openingPointer) latest.current.openingPointer.current = false;
        enablePointer();
      });
    };
    document.addEventListener("pointerup", releasePointer, true);
    document.addEventListener("pointercancel", releasePointer, true);
    const finishExit = () => {
      if (!disposed && !isOpen()) {
        restoreTrigger();
        surface.style.visibility = "hidden";
        gsap.set(node, { autoAlpha: 0 });
        (latest.current.onMotionExit ?? latest.current.popup?.onExited)?.();
      }
    };
    const play = () => context.add(() => {
      const open = isOpen();
      if (open === previousOpen) return;
      const first = previousOpen === undefined;
      previousOpen = open;
      timeline?.kill(); resize?.kill();
      node.inert = !open;
      faceReady = false;
      pointerHeld = open && !!latest.current.openingPointer?.current;
      enablePointer();
      // A primitive can remount its closed popup during the final unmount commit.
      // That instance has no open face to fold; never paint its natural size.
      if (first && !open) {
        restoreTrigger();
        gsap.set(node, { autoAlpha: 0 });
        gsap.set(surface, { visibility: "hidden" });
        return;
      }
      if (motionTone === "hint") {
        if (prefersReducedMotion()) {
          gsap.set(node, { autoAlpha: open ? 1 : 0 });
          if (!open) finishExit();
          return;
        }
        if (open) gsap.set(node, { opacity: 0, scale: 0.98, y: 2 });
        timeline = gsap.timeline({ onComplete: open ? undefined : finishExit });
        timeline.to(node, { autoAlpha: open ? 1 : 0, scale: open ? 1 : 0.98,
          y: open ? 0 : 2, duration: open ? 0.18 : 0.12, ease: "power2.out" });
        return;
      }
      const triggerRect = trigger?.getBoundingClientRect();
      const fromWidth = sideways ? 4 : triggerRect?.width ?? targetWidth * 0.7;
      const fromHeight = triggerRect?.height ?? 32;
      const expanded = dimensions();
      const motion = open ? SHARE_MOTION.open : SHARE_MOTION.close;
      if (trigger && !sideways) { trigger.style.opacity = "0"; ownsTriggerOpacity = true; }
      gsap.set(node, { visibility: "visible", opacity: 1 });
      gsap.set(surface, { visibility: "visible", overflow: "hidden", pointerEvents: open ? "auto" : "none" });
      if (prefersReducedMotion()) {
        gsap.set(surface, { width: open ? expanded.width : fromWidth, height: open ? expanded.height : fromHeight,
          borderRadius: open ? radius : buttonRadius, "--spring-corner-curvature": open ? curvature : buttonCurvature,
          backgroundColor: open ? background : buttonBackground,
          boxShadow: open ? shadow : "none", borderColor: open ? borderColor : "transparent" });
        gsap.set(compact, { opacity: open ? 0 : 1 });
        gsap.set(body, { opacity: open ? 1 : 0, filter: "blur(0px)" });
        place();
        faceReady = open; enablePointer();
        if (!open) finishExit();
        else settleOverflow();
        return;
      }
      if (open && timeline === undefined) {
        gsap.set(surface, { width: fromWidth, height: fromHeight, borderRadius: buttonRadius,
          "--spring-corner-curvature": buttonCurvature,
          backgroundColor: buttonBackground, boxShadow: "none", borderColor: "transparent" });
        gsap.set(compact, { opacity: 1 });
        gsap.set(body, { opacity: 0, filter: "blur(3px)" });
      }
      timeline = gsap.timeline({ onComplete: () => {
        if (!open) { finishExit(); return; }
        settleOverflow();
      } });
      timeline.to(surface, { width: open ? expanded.width : fromWidth,
        duration: motion.width.duration, ease: springEase(motion.width.stiffness, motion.width.damping, motion.width.duration) }, motion.width.delay);
      timeline.to(surface, { height: open ? expanded.height : fromHeight,
        duration: motion.height.duration, ease: springEase(motion.height.stiffness, motion.height.damping, motion.height.duration) }, motion.height.delay);
      timeline.to(surface, { borderRadius: open ? radius : buttonRadius,
        "--spring-corner-curvature": open ? curvature : buttonCurvature,
        backgroundColor: open ? background : buttonBackground, boxShadow: open ? shadow : "none",
        borderColor: open ? borderColor : "transparent", duration: 0.22, ease: "power2.out" }, 0);
      timeline.to(compact, { opacity: open ? 0 : 1, duration: 0.14, ease: "power2.out" }, open ? 0 : 0.2);
      timeline.to(body, { opacity: open ? 1 : 0,
        filter: open ? "blur(0px)" : "blur(3px)", duration: open ? 0.25 : 0.14, ease: "power2.out" }, open ? 0.12 : 0);
      if (open) timeline.call(() => { faceReady = true; enablePointer(); }, [], 0.37);
      if (open) {
        const rows = Array.from(body.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("[role=menuitem], [role=menuitemradio], [data-menu-item]")).slice(0, 24);
        timeline.fromTo(rows, { opacity: 0, y: 4 }, { opacity: 1, y: 0,
          duration: 0.25, stagger: Math.min(0.018, 0.16 / Math.max(1, rows.length)), ease: "power2.out" }, 0.14);
      }
    });
    play(); place();
    if (motionTone === "menu") gsap.ticker.add(place);
    // Floating primitives position their parent after this child's layout effect.
    // Re-anchor before paint when that transform arrives, including production
    // builds where StrictMode does not replay layout effects.
    const positionObserver = new MutationObserver(place);
    if (motionTone === "menu" && node.parentElement) positionObserver.observe(node.parentElement,
      { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["style"] });
    const observer = new MutationObserver(play);
    observer.observe(node, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["data-state", "data-open", "data-closed"] });
    let rendered = dimensions();
    const retarget = () => {
      if (!isOpen() || motionTone === "hint" || disposed) return;
      const width = dimensions().width;
      body.style.width = `${Math.max(0, width - paddingX - borderX)}px`;
      const contentHeight = body.offsetHeight + paddingY + borderY;
      const nextHeight = Number.isFinite(maxHeight) ? Math.min(contentHeight, maxHeight) : contentHeight;
      const contentChanged = Math.abs(nextHeight - targetHeight) >= 1;
      targetHeight = nextHeight;
      const next = dimensions();
      const widthChanged = Math.abs(next.width - rendered.width) >= 1;
      if (!contentChanged && !widthChanged && Math.abs(next.height - rendered.height) < 1) return;
      rendered = next;
      context.add(() => {
        resize?.kill(); gsap.killTweensOf(surface, widthChanged ? "width,height" : "height");
        gsap.set(node, { width, height: targetHeight });
        const m = SHARE_MOTION.resize;
        resize = gsap.to(surface, { ...(widthChanged ? {width} : {}), height: next.height, duration: prefersReducedMotion() ? 0 : m.duration,
          ease: springEase(m.stiffness, m.damping, m.duration),
          onComplete: settleOverflow,
        });
      });
    };
    const contentResize = new ResizeObserver(retarget);
    contentResize.observe(body);
    window.addEventListener("resize", retarget);
    return () => {
      disposed = true;
      observer.disconnect(); contentResize.disconnect(); positionObserver.disconnect();
      gsap.ticker.remove(place);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", retarget);
      document.removeEventListener("pointerup", releasePointer, true);
      document.removeEventListener("pointercancel", releasePointer, true);
      cancelAnimationFrame(releaseFrame);
      restoreTrigger(); compact.replaceChildren();
      timeline?.kill(); resize?.kill();
      context.revert();
      node.style.visibility = "hidden";
      surface.style.visibility = "hidden";
    };
  }, [motionTone]);

  return <div {...props} ref={setRef} data-spring-tone={motionTone} className={`spring-surface ui-menu-surface ${className ?? ""}`}>
    <div ref={surfaceRef} data-spring-shell>
      <div ref={compactRef} aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute" />
      <div ref={bodyRef} className="spring-surface-content">{children}</div>
    </div>
  </div>;
});


/* ===== theme/continuousCorners.ts · SHA256 7cdd2ff0332d8279b4c7161abff0df381b1d0850750090fea53d1d89a2a7e96c ===== */
import type { CSSProperties } from "react";

/** Shared Apple-style corner policy for CSS, inline styles, and animation targets. */
export const CONTINUOUS_CORNERS = Object.freeze({ radius: 24, smoothing: 0.6 });
export const CORNER_RADIUS_SCALE = 1 + CONTINUOUS_CORNERS.smoothing;

// Native superellipses approximate the approved Figma curve. Match its straight-edge
// extent and 45-degree inset rather than replacing it with the squircle keyword.
export const CORNER_CURVATURE = Math.log2(
  -Math.LN2 / Math.log(1 - (1 - Math.SQRT1_2) / CORNER_RADIUS_SCALE),
);

export function supportsContinuousCorners() {
  return typeof CSS !== "undefined" && CSS.supports("corner-shape", "superellipse(2)");
}

export function continuousRadius(radius: number, supported = supportsContinuousCorners()) {
  return supported ? radius * CORNER_RADIUS_SCALE : radius;
}

export function continuousCornerStyle(
  radius: number | string,
  supported = supportsContinuousCorners(),
): CSSProperties & { cornerShape?: string } {
  // These values intentionally describe circles/capsules, including canvas handles.
  if (typeof radius === "string") {
    return radius.includes("%")
      ? { borderRadius: radius, cornerShape: "round" }
      : { borderRadius: supported ? `calc((${radius}) * ${CORNER_RADIUS_SCALE})` : radius };
  }
  if (radius >= 999) return { borderRadius: radius, cornerShape: "round" };
  return { borderRadius: continuousRadius(radius, supported) };
}

export function computedCornerCurvature(style: CSSStyleDeclaration) {
  const shape = style.getPropertyValue("corner-shape");
  if (shape === "round" || !supportsContinuousCorners()) return 1;
  const match = shape.match(/superellipse\(([\d.]+)\)/);
  return match ? Number(match[1]) : CORNER_CURVATURE;
}


/* ===== components/ui/springMotion.ts · SHA256 f2c799c9c0d0850bedc969e6db2b2eb8b98f2c7b45545a9b00cdc1749c6b2ecd ===== */
// One motion source for the reference demo and the production UI.
export const SHARE_MOTION = {
  open: {
    width: { stiffness: 306.25, damping: 28, duration: 0.7, delay: 0 },
    height: { stiffness: 196, damping: 18.34, duration: 0.86, delay: 0.057 },
  },
  close: {
    width: { stiffness: 400, damping: 33, duration: 0.64, delay: 0.07 },
    height: { stiffness: 600.25, damping: 40, duration: 0.62, delay: 0 },
  },
  resize: { stiffness: 280, damping: 24, duration: 0.64 },
} as const;

export function springEase(stiffness: number, damping: number, duration: number) {
  const decay = damping / 2;
  const frequency = Math.sqrt(stiffness - decay * decay);
  return (progress: number) => {
    if (progress === 0 || progress === 1) return progress;
    const time = progress * duration;
    return 1 - Math.exp(-decay * time) *
      (Math.cos(frequency * time) + decay / frequency * Math.sin(frequency * time));
  };
}

export function prefersReducedMotion() {
  return typeof window !== "undefined" && window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
}

/** GSAP's color parser expects RGB/HSL; themes may resolve to OKLab/color(). */
export function motionColor(color: string): string {
  if (/^rgba?\(/i.test(color)) return color;
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = canvas.height = 1;
  const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
  if (!context) return color;
  context.fillStyle = color;
  context.fillRect(0, 0, 1, 1);
  const [red, green, blue, alpha] = context.getImageData(0, 0, 1, 1).data;
  return `rgba(${red}, ${green}, ${blue}, ${alpha / 255})`;
}

export const SOFT_SPRING = { stiffness: 360, damping: 34, duration: 0.42 } as const;

  --ui-menu-border:color-mix(in srgb, var(--theme-border, #dfe2e7) 75%, transparent);

/* An inset outline stays inside the existing geometry and keeps the menu's
   continuous corners without adding a second line or changing its dimensions. */

  border-width:0;
  outline:1px solid var(--ui-menu-border);
  outline-offset:-1px;
}

  outline:none;
}
.spring-surface.ui-menu-surface[data-spring-tone="menu"] > [data-spring-shell] {
  outline:1px solid var(--ui-menu-border);
  outline-offset:-1px;
}


/* ===== components/ui/useHintPositionMotion.ts · SHA256 136307d819ae37fdf35fa3f7438b3a5189205d240e09abfa6f3c7b1344b60677 ===== */
import { useLayoutEffect, useState } from "react";
import gsap from "gsap";
import { prefersReducedMotion } from "./springMotion";

/** Keep the popup mounted; smooth the positioner's anchor changes on a separate
 * wrapper so Base UI positioning and the hint's entrance transform stay intact. */
export function useHintPositionMotion(enabled: boolean) {
  const [positioner, positionerRef] = useState<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const [shift, shiftRef] = useState<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled || !positioner || !shift) return;
    let previous: DOMRect | null = null;
    let tween: gsap.core.Tween | undefined;
    let frame = 0;
    let disposed = false;
    const remember = () => {
      if (disposed) return;
      previous = positioner.getBoundingClientRect();
    };
    // Initial anchor positioning happens after this child's layout effect.
    frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => { frame = requestAnimationFrame(remember); });
    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
      const next = positioner.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (previous && (Math.abs(next.left - previous.left) > .5 || Math.abs(next.top - previous.top) > .5)) {
        const x = previous.left - next.left + Number(gsap.getProperty(shift, "x"));
        const y = previous.top - next.top + Number(gsap.getProperty(shift, "y"));
        tween?.kill();
        gsap.set(shift, { x, y });
        tween = gsap.to(shift, { x: 0, y: 0, duration: prefersReducedMotion() ? 0 : .16, ease: "power2.out" });
      }
      if (previous) previous = next;
    });
    observer.observe(positioner, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["style"] });
    return () => {
      disposed = true;
      cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
      observer.disconnect(); tween?.kill();
      gsap.set(shift, { clearProps: "transform" });
    };
  }, [enabled, positioner, shift]);
  return { positionerRef, shiftRef };
}


/* ===== components/ui/MorphPanelLayoutContext.tsx · SHA256 e83748217bf31c93303d5591e6673937d4abcadba5ea9066c5f0b273e1f29bb2 ===== */
import { createContext, useContext } from "react";

/** Morph 面板是否正在 layout 形变；为 true 时药丸每帧重测以跟随行位 */
export const MorphPanelLayoutContext = createContext(false);

/** Keep a trigger's informational hint out of the way of its active menu. */
export const MenuTriggerOpenContext = createContext(false);

export function useMorphPanelLayoutAnimating() {
  return useContext(MorphPanelLayoutContext);
}


/* ===== components/ContextCapacityIndicator.tsx · SHA256 0278112e28646aeed49a5b9b00a606e5e846528a5afac28e2ab07451771d18f2 ===== */
import { HoverCard, HoverCardContent, HoverCardTrigger } from "./shadcn/hover-card";
import { useMemo, useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { CircularProgressbar, buildStyles } from "react-circular-progressbar";
import { Button } from "./shadcn/button";
import type { ContextUsageSnapshot } from "../types";
import { formatTokens } from "./chat-timeline/format";
import { MOTION } from "./ui/motion";

interface Props {
  snapshot?: ContextUsageSnapshot | null;
  /**
   * Provider-resolved context window for the active model, from the backend
   * capability resolver. Used when there is no snapshot yet (e.g. an empty
   * session) so the denominator is the model's real capacity, never a
   * hardcoded guess. See deepagent-models::ModelCapabilityResolver.
   */
  contextWindow?: number;
  fallbackPromptTokens?: number;
  /** Hide popover while another toolbar overlay is open. */
  popoverSuppressed?: boolean;
  /** Increment to force-close the popover. */
  overlayCloseSignal?: number;
  onPopoverOpenChange?: (open: boolean) => void;
}

function capacityTone(ratio: number, isEmptySession: boolean): string {
  if (isEmptySession) return "var(--theme-text-secondary, #6b7280)";
  if (ratio >= 0.92) return "#dc4b41";
  if (ratio >= 0.8) return "#d99015";
  return "var(--theme-text-secondary, #6b7280)";
}

/** Projected next request; legacy snapshots retain their original fallback. */
export function contextOccupancyTokens(snapshot: ContextUsageSnapshot): number {
  return snapshot.projected_request_tokens ?? snapshot.measured_prompt_tokens ?? snapshot.estimated_prompt_tokens;
}

export function ContextCapacityIndicator({
  snapshot,
  contextWindow: capabilityContextWindow = 0,
  fallbackPromptTokens = 0,
  popoverSuppressed = false,
  overlayCloseSignal = 0,
  onPopoverOpenChange,
}: Props) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const lastOverlayCloseSignal = useRef(overlayCloseSignal);
  const lastReportedOpen = useRef(false);
  // The run's own snapshot wins (the runtime produced it from the same policy),
  // then the provider-resolved capability. Never a hardcoded cap.
  const contextWindow = snapshot?.context_window ?? capabilityContextWindow;
  const hasWindow = contextWindow > 0;
  const usedTokens = snapshot
    ? contextOccupancyTokens(snapshot)
    : Math.max(0, Math.round(fallbackPromptTokens));
  const isEmptySession = !snapshot && usedTokens === 0;
  // Without a denominator there is no meaningful occupancy — show an empty ring
  // rather than dividing by a placeholder.
  const ratio = !hasWindow
    ? 0
    : Math.max(0, Math.min(1, usedTokens / contextWindow));
  const percent = Math.round(ratio * 100);
  const stroke = capacityTone(ratio, isEmptySession);
  const blocks = useMemo(
    () => [...(snapshot?.blocks ?? [])].sort((a, b) => b.tokens - a.tokens),
    [snapshot?.blocks],
  );
  // Legacy snapshots may lack a complete block partition.
  const unattributed = snapshot
    ? Math.max(
        0,
        contextOccupancyTokens(snapshot) -
          blocks.reduce((sum, block) => sum + block.tokens, 0),
      )
    : 0;
  const cacheTotal = (snapshot?.cache_hit_tokens ?? 0) + (snapshot?.cache_miss_tokens ?? 0);
  const cacheRatio =
    snapshot?.cache_hit_ratio ?? (cacheTotal > 0 ? (snapshot?.cache_hit_tokens ?? 0) / cacheTotal : undefined);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (popoverSuppressed && open) setOpen(false);
  }, [popoverSuppressed, open]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (overlayCloseSignal > lastOverlayCloseSignal.current) {
      lastOverlayCloseSignal.current = overlayCloseSignal;
      setOpen(false);
    }
  }, [overlayCloseSignal]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open === lastReportedOpen.current) return;
    lastReportedOpen.current = open;
    onPopoverOpenChange?.(open);
  }, [open, onPopoverOpenChange]);

  const showPopover = open && !popoverSuppressed;

  return (
    <div className="relative flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center">
      <HoverCard open={showPopover} onOpenChange={(next, details) => {
        if (details.reason === "trigger-press") details.cancel();
        else if (!popoverSuppressed) setOpen(next);
      }}>
      <HoverCardTrigger delay={120} closeDelay={150} render={<Button
        variant="ghost"
        size="icon"
        className={`h-8 w-8 rounded-full hover:bg-ui-tint ${MOTION.fast}`}
        aria-label={`Context ${percent}%`}
        onClick={() => {
          if (!popoverSuppressed) setOpen((value) => !value);
        }}
      >
        <span className="block h-4 w-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true">
          <CircularProgressbar
            value={ratio}
            maxValue={1}
            strokeWidth={12.5}
            className="block h-full w-full [&_.CircularProgressbar-path]:transition-[stroke-dashoffset] [&_.CircularProgressbar-path]:duration-300 motion-reduce:[&_.CircularProgressbar-path]:transition-none"
            styles={buildStyles({
              pathColor: stroke,
              trailColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--theme-text-secondary, #6b7280) 30%, var(--theme-bg, #ffffff))",
              strokeLinecap: "round",
            })}
          />
        </span>
      </Button>} />

      <HoverCardContent motionTone="menu" side="top" align="end" className="w-[240px] px-3.5 py-3">
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
            <span className="font-semibold">预计下一次请求</span>
            <span className="text-[18px] font-semibold leading-none">{percent}%</span>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-1.5 text-[13px] text-text-secondary">
            {hasWindow
              ? `${formatTokens(usedTokens)} / ${formatTokens(contextWindow)}`
              : formatTokens(usedTokens)}
          </div>

          {snapshot?.last_request_prompt_tokens != null && (
            <div className="mt-1.5 flex justify-between gap-2 text-[12px] text-text-secondary">
              <span>上一次请求</span><span>{formatTokens(snapshot.last_request_prompt_tokens)}</span>
            </div>
          )}
          {!!snapshot?.compaction_threshold && (
            <div className="mt-1.5 flex justify-between gap-2 text-[12px] text-text-secondary">
              <span>自动压缩阈值</span><span>{formatTokens(snapshot.compaction_threshold)}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {blocks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-2 space-y-1.5 border-t border-border-theme pt-2">
              <div className="text-[11px] text-text-tertiary">上下文组成（估算）</div>
              {blocks.map((block) => (
                <div key={`${block.kind}-${block.source}`} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <span className="min-w-0 truncate text-text-secondary">{block.name}</span>
                  <span className="shrink-0 font-medium">{formatTokens(block.tokens)}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
              {unattributed > 0 && (
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <span className="min-w-0 truncate text-text-secondary">其他/未归类</span>
                  <span className="shrink-0 font-medium">{formatTokens(unattributed)}</span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {(cacheRatio != null || snapshot?.cache_hit_tokens) && (
            <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-border-theme pt-2 text-text-secondary">
              <span>上次缓存命中</span>
              <span className="font-medium text-text-base">
                {cacheRatio == null ? formatTokens(snapshot?.cache_hit_tokens ?? 0) : `${Math.round(cacheRatio * 100)}%`}
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
      </HoverCardContent>
      </HoverCard>
    </div>
  );
}


/* ===== components/shadcn/button.tsx · SHA256 fcb6ec544f5f3d2fe534bbedac5a4a429a3feb9b35dcb264af2c6dc5978d5ba9 ===== */
import * as React from "react";

import { cn } from "./utils";
import { useSpringPress } from "../ui/useSpringPress";

type ButtonVariant = "default" | "secondary" | "outline" | "ghost" | "destructive";
type ButtonSize = "default" | "sm" | "icon";

export interface ButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: ButtonSize;
}

const variantClasses: Record<ButtonVariant, string> = {
  default: "bg-text-base text-white shadow-sm hover:bg-black",
  secondary: "bg-gray-100 text-text-base hover:bg-gray-200",
  outline: "border border-border-theme bg-white text-text-base shadow-sm hover:bg-gray-50",
  ghost: "text-text-secondary hover:bg-gray-100 hover:text-text-base",
  destructive: "bg-red-600 text-white shadow-sm hover:bg-red-700",
};

const sizeClasses: Record<ButtonSize, string> = {
  default: "h-9 px-3.5 py-2 text-[13px]",
  sm: "h-8 px-3 text-[12px]",
  icon: "h-9 w-9 p-0",
};

const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant = "default", size = "default", type = "button", ...props }, ref) => {
    const pressRef = useSpringPress(ref);
    return (
    <button
      ref={pressRef}
      type={type}
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg font-medium outline-none transition-colors focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary/30 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-45",
        variantClasses[variant],
        sizeClasses[size],
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
    );
  },
);
Button.displayName = "Button";

export { Button };


/* ===== components/ui/useSpringPress.ts · SHA256 e597399ef7eccf2572b7d7d8d30e6529ff4dcfb1a7e9f08f371cab00313b4143 ===== */
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, type ForwardedRef } from "react";
import gsap from "gsap";
import { prefersReducedMotion, springEase } from "./springMotion";

/** Pointer press/release only: never animate a hover or move an icon separately. */
export function useSpringPress<T extends HTMLElement>(forwardedRef?: ForwardedRef<T>) {
  const local = useRef<T | null>(null);
  const ref = useCallback((node: T | null) => {
    local.current = node;
    if (typeof forwardedRef === "function") forwardedRef(node);
    else if (forwardedRef) forwardedRef.current = node;
  }, [forwardedRef]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const node = local.current;
    if (!node) return;
    let pressed = false;
    let tween: gsap.core.Tween | undefined;
    const release = () => {
      if (!pressed) return;
      pressed = false;
      tween?.kill();
      tween = gsap.to(node, { scale: 1, duration: prefersReducedMotion() ? 0 : 0.38,
        ease: springEase(420, 24, 0.38) });
    };
    const down = () => {
      if (node.matches(":disabled") || prefersReducedMotion()) return;
      pressed = true; tween?.kill();
      tween = gsap.to(node, { scale: 0.97, duration: 0.075, ease: "power2.out" });
    };
    node.addEventListener("pointerdown", down);
    window.addEventListener("pointerup", release);
    window.addEventListener("pointercancel", release);
    window.addEventListener("blur", release);
    return () => {
      node.removeEventListener("pointerdown", down);
      window.removeEventListener("pointerup", release);
      window.removeEventListener("pointercancel", release);
      window.removeEventListener("blur", release);
      tween?.kill(); gsap.set(node, { clearProps: "scale" });
    };
  }, []);
  return ref;
}


/* ===== components/chat-timeline/format.ts · SHA256 44e1335aeac093927315c9fa1029a97f024e640cc23e9c040f3690221c68c6ce ===== */
const CNY_SYMBOL = "\uFFE5";

export function formatMs(ms: number): string {
  if (ms < 1000) return `${ms}ms`;
  const seconds = ms / 1000;
  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds.toFixed(1)}s`;
  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
  const rest = Math.round(seconds % 60);
  return `${minutes}m${rest}s`;
}

/**
 * The app's one token formatter: the per-turn footer, the session totals bar and
 * the context-capacity ring all render the same number, so they must not carry
 * separate rounding rules (they previously disagreed — `16k` vs `15.8k`).
 */
export function formatTokens(value: number): string {
  const n = Math.max(0, Math.round(value));
  if (n < 1000) return `${n}`;
  // 999_500 rounds to "1M" rather than the "1000k" a plain 1e6 cutoff produces.
  if (n < 999_500) return `${formatUnit(n / 1000)}k`;
  return `${formatUnit(n / 1_000_000)}M`;
}

/** One decimal place, without a trailing `.0`. */
function formatUnit(value: number): string {
  const rounded = Math.round(value * 10) / 10;
  return Number.isInteger(rounded) ? String(rounded) : rounded.toFixed(1);
}

export function formatCny(n: number): string {
  if (n <= 0) return `${CNY_SYMBOL}0`;
  if (n < 0.01) return `${CNY_SYMBOL}${n.toFixed(6)}`;
  if (n < 1) return `${CNY_SYMBOL}${n.toFixed(4)}`;
  return `${CNY_SYMBOL}${n.toFixed(2)}`;
}

export const cnySymbol = CNY_SYMBOL;


/* ===== components/ui/motion.ts · SHA256 b9bdc69fce350cefb8676c959bf51b10cb0f2ca8b461136cc8343ac3b357fa9f ===== */
/**
 * 统一动效语言（全局唯一来源）：
 * - fast：150ms —— 按钮、列表行等小元素的 hover/激活反馈
 * - standard：300ms ease-out（cubic-bezier(0.4,0,0.2,1)）—— 滑块、面板等中等动效
 * - smooth：400ms —— 大面板展开、较大过渡
 * ease-out 即 Tailwind 默认的 cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)。
 */
export const MOTION = {
  fast: "transition-colors duration-150",
  standard: "transition-all duration-300 ease-out",
  smooth: "transition-all duration-[400ms] ease-out",
} as const;

/** Selected navigation feel: one interruptible highlight, with a short exit. */
export const NAVIGATION_MOTION = {
  duration: 0.22,
  ease: "back.out(0.7)",
  exitDuration: 0.07,
  enterDuration: 0.055,
  tooltipDelay: 160,
} as const;

/** 二级浮层菜单（A 视觉 + H 动效）—— 配合 styles.css 中 .floating-menu-panel */

  row:
    "floating-menu-item flex w-full cursor-pointer items-center rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-[12px] transition-colors duration-150 hover:bg-ui-tint",
} as const;

export const MENU_LIST = {
  block: "p-2",
  gutter: "px-1.5 py-1",
  /** 滑动药丸内缩 —— 与 shell p-1.5 叠加，行 hover 不贴边 */

  search: "flex items-center text-text-secondary",

  searchBar: "flex items-center text-[13px] text-text-secondary",

} as const;


/* ===== components/ComposerSuggestPanel.tsx · SHA256 ce190e949289b7d82e2eccb90acbe71d022158b2e5da88902e7f30b67ab57e0d ===== */
import { useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { Panel } from "./ui/Panel";
import { HoverInfo } from "./ui/HoverInfo";
import { MENU_ITEM_ATTR, SlidingMenuList } from "./ui/SlidingMenuList";

export type ComposerSuggestSection<T> = {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  items: T[];
};



interface Props<T> {
  id: string;
  open: boolean;
  sections: ComposerSuggestSection<T>[];
  selectedIndex: number;
  getKey: (item: T) => string;
  renderItem: (item: T, selected: boolean) => ReactNode;
  renderTooltip?: (item: T) => ReactNode;
  onSelect: (item: T) => void;
  onHover: (index: number) => void;
  className?: string;
}

export function ComposerSuggestPanel<T>({
  id,
  open,
  sections,
  selectedIndex,
  getKey,
  renderItem,
  renderTooltip,
  onSelect,
  onHover,
  className = "",
}: Props<T>) {
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const activeId = useMemo(() => {
    let index = 0;
    for (const section of sections) {
      for (const item of section.items) {
        if (index === selectedIndex) return getKey(item);
        index += 1;
      }
    }
    return "";
  }, [sections, selectedIndex, getKey]);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const panel = panelRef.current;
    const anchor = panel?.parentElement;
    if (!open || !panel || !anchor) return;
    const resize = () => {
      const top = anchor.closest(".app-main-surface")?.getBoundingClientRect().top ?? 0;
      // Leave 8px above/below the shell, plus its two 6px padding gutters.
      const available = Math.max(0, anchor.getBoundingClientRect().top - Math.max(0, top) - 28);
      panel.style.setProperty("--suggest-list-height", `${Math.min(320, available)}px`);
    };
    resize();
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(resize);
    observer.observe(anchor);
    window.addEventListener("resize", resize);
    return () => { observer.disconnect(); window.removeEventListener("resize", resize); };
  }, [open]);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const scroll = scrollRef.current;
    const selected = scroll?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-selected="true"]');
    if (!open || !scroll || !selected) return;
    const bounds = scroll.getBoundingClientRect(), row = selected.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (row.top < bounds.top) scroll.scrollTop -= bounds.top - row.top;
    else if (row.bottom > bounds.bottom) scroll.scrollTop += row.bottom - bounds.bottom;
  }, [open, activeId]);

  if (!open) return null;
  return (
    <>
      <Panel
        ref={panelRef}
        data-composer-suggest
        className={`absolute inset-x-0 bottom-full z-50 mb-2 w-auto overflow-hidden p-1.5 ${className}`}
      >
      <div ref={scrollRef} className="composer-suggest-scroll custom-scrollbar max-h-[var(--suggest-list-height,240px)] overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain">
      <SlidingMenuList id={id} role="listbox" aria-label={sections.map(section => section.label).join("、")} activeId={activeId} pillClassName="left-0 right-0">
        {sections.map((section, sectionIndex) => {
          let baseIndex = 0;
          for (let i = 0; i < sectionIndex; i += 1) {
            baseIndex += sections[i].items.length;
          }
          return (
            <div key={section.key} className={sectionIndex > 0 ? "mt-1" : ""}>
              <div className="px-2 py-1.5 text-[12px] font-medium text-text-secondary">
                {section.label}
              </div>
              {section.items.map((item, index) => {
                const flatIndex = baseIndex + index;
                const selected = flatIndex === selectedIndex;
                const itemKey = getKey(item);
                const button = (
                  <button
                    key={itemKey}
                    type="button"
                    role="option"
                    id={`${id}-option-${flatIndex}`}
                    aria-selected={selected}
                    tabIndex={-1}
                    {...{ [MENU_ITEM_ATTR]: itemKey }}
                    onMouseDown={(event) => {
                      event.preventDefault();
                      onSelect(item);
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={() => onHover(flatIndex)}
                    className="relative z-[1] w-full text-left"
                  >
                    {renderItem(item, selected)}
                  </button>
                );
                return renderTooltip ? (
                  <HoverInfo key={itemKey} content={renderTooltip(item)}>{button}</HoverInfo>
                ) : (
                  button
                );
              })}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </SlidingMenuList>
      </div>
      </Panel>
    </>
  );
}


/* ===== components/ui/Panel.tsx · SHA256 bd91784bf10998950b2fb823c5e1c9bd49969c2b3599ae0888d0123397a232b2 ===== */
import { forwardRef, type HTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../shadcn/utils";
import { FLOATING_MENU } from "./motion";

/**
 * 浮层容器（下拉菜单/弹层面板）—— 浅色细边线、bg-elevated-bg + 柔和阴影。
 * menu=true 时附加入场动效（方案 H）；模态框等传 menu={false}。
 */
export const Panel = forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> & { menu?: boolean }>(
  function Panel({ className, menu = true, ...rest }, ref) {
    return (
      <div
        ref={ref}
        className={cn(
          "rounded-2xl bg-elevated-bg shadow-[0_6px_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.10)]",
          menu && FLOATING_MENU.panel,
          className,
        )}
        {...rest}
      />
    );
  },
);


/* ===== components/ui/SlidingMenuList.tsx · SHA256 0b94108f7a0397b9cd17b70655a7b88404428516f1c7ff7c91ed398f9520f7b2 ===== */
import type { HTMLAttributes, ReactNode } from "react";
import { cn } from "../shadcn/utils";
import { MENU_LIST } from "./motion";
import { useSlidingIndicator, SlidingPill } from "./SlidingPill";

/** 浮层菜单行统一 data 属性，配合 SlidingMenuList 使用 */
export const MENU_ITEM_ATTR = "data-menu-item";

type Props = HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> & {
  activeId: string;
  pillClassName?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
};

/**
 * 浮层内列表滑动药丸 —— 与侧栏/设置侧栏同款交互：
 * 悬停跟随，离开滑回当前选中项；行元素须带 `data-menu-item={id}` 与 `relative z-[1]`。
 */
export function SlidingMenuList({
  activeId,
  pillClassName,
  className,
  children,
  ...rest
}: Props) {
  const { containerRef, containerProps, indicatorStyle } = useSlidingIndicator({
    hoverSelector: `[${MENU_ITEM_ATTR}]`,
    activeSelector: `[${MENU_ITEM_ATTR}="${activeId}"]`,
  });

  return (
    <div ref={containerRef} {...containerProps} className={cn("relative w-full overflow-hidden", className)} {...rest}>
      {children}
      <SlidingPill
        className={cn("rounded-lg", MENU_LIST.pillInset, pillClassName)}
        style={indicatorStyle}
      />
    </div>
  );
}


/* ===== components/ui/SlidingPill.tsx · SHA256 51272803185667843f31840c32128c742d8d59947f9fbcfa4e29c365f78833ff ===== */
import { useCallback, useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState, type CSSProperties, type FocusEvent, type MouseEvent } from "react";
import gsap from "gsap";
import { cn } from "../shadcn/utils";
import { MOTION } from "./motion";

/**
 * 滑动指示器（静默着色药丸）—— 统一侧栏/导航的悬停滑动交互。
 *
 * 用法：
 *   const { containerRef, containerProps, indicatorStyle } = useSlidingIndicator({
 *     hoverSelector: "[data-nav]",            // 悬停时药丸跟随的目标
 *     activeSelector: `[data-nav="${active}"]` // 鼠标离开后药丸停靠的位置
 *   });
 *   return (
 *     <div ref={containerRef} {...containerProps} className="relative ...">
 *       ...按钮（须带 data-* 属性与 z-[1]）...
 *       <SlidingPill style={indicatorStyle} />
 *     </div>
 *   );
 *
 * 注意：容器内按钮必须 `relative z-[1]`（在药丸 z-0 之上），否则药丸会遮住文字。
 *
 * 测量用布局坐标（offsetTop/offsetHeight）而非 getBoundingClientRect：
 * morph 形变中容器被 framer-motion 以 transform: scale 变换，rect 返回被
 * 缩放的视口坐标（实测行高被压缩数倍），offset* 不受 transform 影响，
 * 任何时刻都返回真实布局值，因此药丸在面板打开瞬间即可正确显示。
 */
export function useSlidingIndicator({
  hoverSelector,
  activeSelector,
  followFocus = false,
}: {
  hoverSelector: string;
  activeSelector: string;
  followFocus?: boolean;
}) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const hoveredRef = useRef<Element | null>(null);
  const focusedRef = useRef<Element | null>(null);
  const [pill, setPill] = useState<{ top: number; height: number } | null>(null);
  /* 首帧禁用过渡：面板打开时滑块直接以最终形态出现（跳过 height 0→67 的过渡），
     下一帧后恢复 class 过渡，hover 跟随/切换行仍平滑 */
  const [settled, setSettled] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => setSettled(true), 0);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, []);

  const positionPillOn = useCallback((el: Element | null | undefined) => {
    if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    const container = containerRef.current;
    if (!container) return;
    setPill({
      top: el.offsetTop + container.scrollTop,
      height: el.offsetHeight,
    });
  }, []);

  const positionOnActive = useCallback(() => {
    const c = containerRef.current;
    if (!c) return;
    const el = c.querySelector(activeSelector);
    if (!el) {
      setPill(null);
      return;
    }
    positionPillOn(el);
  }, [activeSelector, positionPillOn]);

  const repositionPill = useCallback(() => {
    const target = hoveredRef.current ?? focusedRef.current;
    if (target) {
      positionPillOn(target);
    } else {
      positionOnActive();
    }
  }, [positionOnActive, positionPillOn]);

  /* 挂载 & 激活项变化时，药丸滑到激活项。
     useLayoutEffect：面板打开的首帧（paint 前）就同步测好位置，
     滑块直接以最终形态出现，不会先渲染空状态再过渡 */
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (followFocus) repositionPill();
    else positionOnActive();
  }, [followFocus, positionOnActive, repositionPill]);

  /* 容器尺寸变化时重新测量（hover 时跟 hover 行） */
  useEffect(() => {
    const container = containerRef.current;
    if (!container) return;
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(() => repositionPill());
    ro.observe(container);
    return () => ro.disconnect();
  }, [repositionPill]);

  const handleMouseOver = useCallback(
    (e: MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
      const btn = (e.target as HTMLElement).closest(hoverSelector);
      if (btn) {
        hoveredRef.current = btn;
        positionPillOn(btn);
      }
    },
    [hoverSelector, positionPillOn],
  );

  const handleMouseLeave = useCallback(() => {
    hoveredRef.current = null;
    repositionPill();
  }, [repositionPill]);

  const handleFocus = useCallback((event: FocusEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    const target = event.target.closest(hoverSelector);
    focusedRef.current = target?.matches(":focus-visible") ? target : null;
    if (focusedRef.current) hoveredRef.current = null;
    repositionPill();
  }, [hoverSelector, repositionPill]);

  const handleBlur = useCallback((event: FocusEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (!event.currentTarget.contains(event.relatedTarget)) {
      focusedRef.current = null;
      repositionPill();
    }
  }, [repositionPill]);

  return {
    containerRef,
    containerProps: {
      onMouseOver: handleMouseOver, onMouseLeave: handleMouseLeave,
      onFocusCapture: followFocus ? handleFocus : undefined,
      onBlurCapture: followFocus ? handleBlur : undefined,
    },
    indicatorStyle: {
      top: pill?.top ?? 0,
      height: pill?.height ?? 0,
      opacity: pill ? 1 : 0,
      /* 首帧无过渡（直接显示最终形态），settled 后走 class 的 transition-all */
      transition: settled ? undefined : "none",
    } as CSSProperties,
  };
}

/** 静默着色药丸 —— 默认 ui-tint；侧栏传 className="bg-sidebar-highlight" */
const PILL_BASE = `ui-hover-highlight absolute left-3 right-3 z-0 rounded-md pointer-events-none ${MOTION.standard}`;

type PillMotion = { duration: number; ease: string; exitDuration: number; enterDuration: number };
type PillTarget = { top: number; height: number; visible: boolean; immediate: boolean };

function createPillMotion(node: HTMLDivElement, motion: PillMotion) {
  const media = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
  const context = gsap.context(() => {}, node);
  let target: PillTarget = { top: 0, height: 0, visible: false, immediate: true };
  const paint = () => context.add(() => {
    gsap.killTweensOf(node);
    if (media.matches || target.immediate) {
      gsap.set(node, { y: target.top, height: target.height, opacity: target.visible ? 1 : 0 });
    } else if (!target.visible) {
      gsap.to(node, { opacity: 0, duration: motion.exitDuration, overwrite: true });
    } else if (Number(gsap.getProperty(node, "opacity")) <= .01) {
      gsap.set(node, { y: target.top, height: target.height, opacity: 0 });
      gsap.to(node, { opacity: 1, duration: motion.enterDuration, overwrite: true });
    } else {
      gsap.to(node, { y: target.top, height: target.height, opacity: 1,
        duration: motion.duration, ease: motion.ease, overwrite: true });
    }
  });
  const reduce = () => {
    gsap.killTweensOf(node);
    context.add(() => gsap.set(node, { y: target.top, height: target.height, opacity: target.visible ? 1 : 0 }));
  };
  media.addEventListener("change", reduce);
  return {
    moveTo(next: PillTarget) { target = next; paint(); },
    dispose() { media.removeEventListener("change", reduce); context.revert(); },
  };
}

export function SlidingPill({ style, className, motion }: { style: CSSProperties; className?: string; motion?: PillMotion }) {
  const nodeRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const controller = useRef<ReturnType<typeof createPillMotion> | null>(null);
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!motion || !nodeRef.current) return;
    const instance = createPillMotion(nodeRef.current, motion);
    controller.current = instance;
    return () => { instance.dispose(); controller.current = null; };
  }, [motion]);
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    controller.current?.moveTo({ top: Number(style.top ?? 0), height: Number(style.height ?? 0),
      visible: Number(style.opacity ?? 0) > 0, immediate: style.transition === "none" });
  }, [motion, style.top, style.height, style.opacity, style.transition]);
  const hasBgClass = Boolean(className && /\bbg-/.test(className));
  const { top: _top, height: _height, opacity: _opacity, ...rest } = style;
  return (
    <div
      ref={nodeRef}
      aria-hidden
      className={cn(PILL_BASE, !hasBgClass && "bg-ui-tint", className, motion && "transition-none")}
      style={motion ? { ...rest, top: 0, transition: "none", willChange: "transform" } : style}
    />
  );
}

/* ===== components/ui/EffortSlider.tsx · SHA256 d5f6bc4a829aa2fdca121677f6c2f3ac0257a820700012c7cdd16fb80659efa3 ===== */
import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Slider as SliderPrimitive } from "../shadcn/slider";
import { Button } from "../shadcn/button";
import type { SliderStop } from "./Slider";
import { createEffortMotion } from "./effortMotion";

type Gesture = { id: number; startX: number; lastX: number; time: number; moved: boolean };

type Props<T extends string> = {
  stops: SliderStop<T>[];
  value: T;
  onChange: (value: T) => void;
  ariaLabel: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
};

export function EffortSlider<T extends string>({ stops, value, onChange, ariaLabel, disabled }: Props<T>) {
  const [reducedMotion, setReducedMotion] = useState(() => window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches);
  const active = Math.max(0, stops.findIndex(stop => stop.value === value));
  const max = Math.max(0, stops.length - 1);
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState(active);
  const [dragging, setDragging] = useState(false);
  const previewRef = useRef(active);
  const draggingRef = useRef(false);
  const gesture = useRef<Gesture | null>(null);
  const rootRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const motion = useRef<ReturnType<typeof createEffortMotion> | null>(null);
  const selected = Math.min(max, Math.max(0, Math.round(preview)));
  const ratio = max ? preview / max : 0;

  const updatePreview = (next: number) => {
    previewRef.current = Math.max(0, Math.min(max, next));
    setPreview(previewRef.current);
  };
  const commit = (next: number) => {
    const index = Math.max(0, Math.min(max, Math.round(next)));
    updatePreview(index);
    if (stops[index] && stops[index].value !== value) onChange(stops[index].value);
  };
  const cancel = () => {
    draggingRef.current = false;
    gesture.current = null;
    setDragging(false);
    updatePreview(active);
    motion.current?.release();
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const media = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    const update = () => setReducedMotion(media.matches);
    update();
    media.addEventListener("change", update);
    return () => media.removeEventListener("change", update);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Respect optimistic updates and a parent's rollback after a failed save.
    if (!draggingRef.current) {
      previewRef.current = active;
      setPreview(active);
    }
  }, [active]);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const root = rootRef.current;
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!root || !canvas) return;
    const controller = createEffortMotion(root, canvas, ratio, reducedMotion);
    motion.current = controller;
    return () => {
      controller.dispose();
      motion.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  useLayoutEffect(() => { motion.current?.moveTo(ratio, dragging); }, [ratio, dragging]);
  useLayoutEffect(() => { motion.current?.setReducedMotion(reducedMotion); }, [reducedMotion]);

  return (
    <div ref={rootRef} className="reasoning-effort" data-dragging={dragging || undefined}
      data-maximum={selected === max || undefined} data-disabled={disabled || undefined}>
      <SliderPrimitive appearance="effort" min={0} max={Math.max(1, max)} step={0.001}
        value={[preview]} disabled={disabled || max === 0}
        thumbProps={{ "aria-label": ariaLabel, "aria-valuenow": selected, "aria-valuetext": stops[selected]?.label }}
        onValueChange={([next]) => updatePreview(next)} onValueCommit={([next]) => commit(next)}
        onPointerDown={event => { if (!event.isPrimary || event.button !== 0) event.preventDefault(); }}
        onPointerDownCapture={event => {
          if (disabled || max === 0 || !event.isPrimary || event.button !== 0) return;
          gesture.current = { id: event.pointerId, startX: event.clientX, lastX: event.clientX, time: event.timeStamp, moved: false };
          motion.current?.press();
        }}
        onPointerMoveCapture={event => {
          const current = gesture.current;
          if (!current || event.pointerId !== current.id) return;
          if (!current.moved && Math.abs(event.clientX - current.startX) < 4) return;
          current.moved = true;
          draggingRef.current = true;
          setDragging(true);
          const velocity = (event.clientX - current.lastX) / Math.max(8, event.timeStamp - current.time);
          motion.current?.drag(velocity);
          current.lastX = event.clientX;
          current.time = event.timeStamp;
        }}
        onPointerUpCapture={event => {
          if (event.pointerId !== gesture.current?.id) return;
          gesture.current = null;
          draggingRef.current = false;
          setDragging(false);
          updatePreview(Math.round(previewRef.current));
          motion.current?.release();
        }}
        onPointerCancel={cancel}
        onLostPointerCapture={() => { if (gesture.current) cancel(); }}
        onKeyDownCapture={event => {
          let next = selected;
          if (["ArrowRight", "ArrowUp", "PageUp"].includes(event.key)) next++;
          else if (["ArrowLeft", "ArrowDown", "PageDown"].includes(event.key)) next--;
          else if (event.key === "Home") next = 0;
          else if (event.key === "End") next = max;
          else return;
          event.preventDefault();
          event.stopPropagation();
          if (!disabled) commit(next);
        }}>
        <div className="reasoning-effort-fill" aria-hidden="true"><canvas ref={canvasRef} /></div>
        <div className="reasoning-effort-dots" aria-hidden="true">
          {stops.map(stop => <span key={stop.value} />)}
        </div>
        <span className="reasoning-effort-thumb" aria-hidden="true" />
      </SliderPrimitive>
      <div className="reasoning-effort-stops">
        {stops.map((stop, index) => (
          <Button key={stop.value} variant="ghost" size="sm" disabled={disabled} aria-pressed={index === selected}
            className="h-auto rounded px-0.5 py-[3px] text-[11px] font-normal hover:bg-transparent"
            onClick={() => commit(index)}>{stop.label}</Button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


/* ===== components/shadcn/slider.tsx · SHA256 574612f1a7ae603fccae8023a351adc1a8ddf9f9e2a6c7e8fb8d06c3010d4d22 ===== */

import * as SliderPrimitive from "@radix-ui/react-slider";

const Slider = React.forwardRef<

/* ===== components/ui/effortMotion.ts · SHA256 49f87a7098c84e2e18ef520398c54eccace5d74ceef5a15a964cc088c5852bb8 ===== */
import { gsap } from "gsap";
import { createEffortField } from "./effortField";
import { springEase } from "./springMotion";

// Position keeps A's measured, non-overshooting 500 ms glide.
const SPRING = [[0, 0], [.02, .015], [.04, .0532], [.06, .1065], [.08, .1686],
  [.1, .2351], [.13, .3363], [.16, .4332], [.2, .5495], [.24, .648], [.28, .7287],
  [.33, .807], [.38, .8646], [.44, .9128], [.5, .9447], [.57, .968], [.65, .9834],
  [.74, .9924], [.85, .9975], [1, 1]];
function glideEase(progress: number) {
  const end = SPRING.findIndex(([x]) => x >= progress);
  if (end <= 0) return end === 0 ? 0 : 1;
  const [x0, y0] = SPRING[end - 1];
  const [x1, y1] = SPRING[end];
  return y0 + (y1 - y0) * (progress - x0) / (x1 - x0);
}

// Local design: material deformation springs back while the selected stop stays fixed.
const returnEase = springEase(400, 23, 0.72);

/** One motion implementation shared by the desktop component and its review file. */
export function createEffortMotion(
  root: HTMLElement,
  canvas: HTMLCanvasElement,
  initialRatio: number,
  initialReducedMotion: boolean,
) {
  const state = { ratio: initialRatio, press: 1, body: 0, lean: 0, boost: 1 };
  let targetRatio = initialRatio;
  let reduced = initialReducedMotion;
  let attached = false;
  let disposed = false;
  let positionTween: gsap.core.Tween | undefined;
  let pressTween: gsap.core.Tween | undefined;
  let shapeTween: gsap.core.Timeline | gsap.core.Tween | undefined;
  let boostTween: gsap.core.Tween | undefined;
  const field = createEffortField(canvas, () => state.ratio);
  const variables = () => ({
    "--effort-ratio": String(state.ratio),
    "--effort-press": String(state.press),
    "--effort-jelly-x": String(1 + state.body),
    "--effort-jelly-y": String(1 - state.body * 0.3),
    "--effort-jelly-offset": `${-state.lean * 9}px`,
    "--effort-jelly-skew": `${-state.lean * 12}deg`,
    "--effort-jelly-left": `${50 + state.lean * 32}%`,
    "--effort-jelly-right": `${50 - state.lean * 24}%`,
  });
  const context = gsap.context(() => { gsap.set(root, variables()); }, root);
  const paint = () => {
    if (disposed) return;
    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(variables())) root.style.setProperty(name, value);
    if (reduced) field.draw(gsap.ticker.time, true);
  };
  const draw = (time: number) => {
    if (!document.hidden) field.draw(time, false, state.boost);
  };
  const syncTicker = () => {
    if (attached) gsap.ticker.remove(draw);
    attached = !reduced;
    field.draw(gsap.ticker.time, true);
    if (attached) gsap.ticker.add(draw);
  };
  const stopTweens = () => {
    positionTween?.kill();
    pressTween?.kill();
    shapeTween?.kill();
    boostTween?.kill();
  };
  syncTicker();

  return {
    moveTo(ratio: number, immediate: boolean) {
      targetRatio = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ratio));
      positionTween?.kill();
      positionTween = gsap.to(state, {
        ratio: targetRatio, duration: immediate || reduced ? 0 : 0.5,
        ease: glideEase, onUpdate: paint,
      });
    },
    press() {
      pressTween?.kill();
      pressTween = gsap.to(state, { press: reduced ? 1 : 0.96, duration: reduced ? 0 : 0.08, onUpdate: paint });
    },
    drag(velocity: number) {
      if (reduced || disposed) return;
      const speed = Math.min(3.5, Math.abs(velocity));
      const deformation = Math.min(0.46, 0.045 + speed * 0.17);
      const lean = Math.sign(velocity) * deformation;
      shapeTween?.kill();
      // On reversal, lean crosses through its previous value instead of flipping instantly.
      // When the pointer pauses, the same material relaxes without waiting for pointerup.
      shapeTween = gsap.timeline({ onUpdate: paint })
        .to(state, { body: deformation, lean, duration: 0.09, ease: "power2.out" })
        .to(state, { body: 0, lean: 0, duration: 0.72, ease: returnEase }, ">0.07");
      boostTween?.kill();
      boostTween = gsap.to(state, {
        boost: 1 + Math.min(2.2, 0.55 + speed * 0.6), duration: 0.12,
        ease: "power2.out",
      });
    },
    release() {
      pressTween?.kill();
      shapeTween?.kill();
      boostTween?.kill();
      pressTween = gsap.to(state, { press: 1, duration: reduced ? 0 : 0.32, ease: "power2.out", onUpdate: paint });
      shapeTween = gsap.to(state, {
        body: 0, lean: 0, duration: reduced ? 0 : 0.72, ease: returnEase, onUpdate: paint,
      });
      boostTween = gsap.to(state, { boost: 1, duration: reduced ? 0 : 0.7, ease: "power2.out" });
    },
    setReducedMotion(next: boolean) {
      if (next === reduced) return;
      reduced = next;
      stopTweens();
      Object.assign(state, { ratio: targetRatio, press: 1, body: 0, lean: 0, boost: 1 });
      paint();
      syncTicker();
    },
    dispose() {
      disposed = true;
      stopTweens();
      gsap.ticker.remove(draw);
      field.dispose();
      context.revert();
    },
  };
}


/* ===== components/ui/effortField.ts · SHA256 5518873bb82caae654895b423e6e36fdccbcfb223d07c2d2bbc8c717090169d0 ===== */
/** Canvas field recreated from the public Hexta Models preview, without its Pro source. */
export function createEffortField(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement, getRatio: () => number) {
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  let width = 0;
  let height = 0;
  let phase = 0;
  let lastTime = 0;
  const stars = Array.from({ length: 64 }, (_, i) => ({
    x: ((i * 0.61803398875) % 1),
    y: ((i * 0.754877666) % 1),
    size: 0.35 + (i % 5) * 0.13,
    speed: 0.3 + (i % 7) * 0.1,
  }));

  const resize = () => {
    // The enclosing model menu morphs with a CSS transform; use layout pixels.
    width = canvas.clientWidth;
    height = canvas.clientHeight;
    const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(width * dpr));
    canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(height * dpr));
    ctx?.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
  };

  const draw = (time = 0, still = false, motionBoost = 1) => {
    if (!ctx || width <= 0 || height <= 0) return;
    const ratio = getRatio();
    // Integrate speed so changing effort never jumps the particles' positions.
    if (!still) phase += Math.min(Math.max(time - lastTime, 0), 0.05)
      * (0.16 + ratio * 0.85) * Math.max(1, motionBoost);
    lastTime = time;
    const hue = 210 + ratio * ratio * 63;
    const fill = 32 + (width - 32) * ratio;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
    const base = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, Math.max(fill, 32), 0);
    base.addColorStop(0, "#30343a");
    base.addColorStop(0.28, `hsl(${hue - 12} 38% 47%)`);
    base.addColorStop(0.65, `hsl(${hue} 79% 70%)`);
    base.addColorStop(1, `hsl(${hue + 5} 88% 70%)`);
    ctx.fillStyle = base;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "screen";
    // Soft overlapping ribbons create the drifting, diagonal nebula texture.
    for (let band = 0; band < 7; band++) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      for (let x = 0; x <= width + 4; x += 4) {
        const y = height * (0.12 + band * 0.15)
          + Math.sin(x * 0.034 + phase + band * 1.8) * 5
          + Math.sin(x * 0.079 - phase * 0.6 + band) * 2 - x * 0.035;
        if (x === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
        else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
      }
      ctx.lineWidth = 2 + (band % 3) * 2;
      ctx.strokeStyle = `hsla(${hue + band * 2} 90% 87% / ${0.06 + ratio * 0.025})`;
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    const glowX = fill * (0.6 + Math.sin(phase * 0.7) * 0.12);
    const glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(glowX, height * 0.7, 0, glowX, height * 0.7, 46);
    glow.addColorStop(0, `hsla(${hue - 12} 100% 88% / .35)`);
    glow.addColorStop(1, `hsla(${hue} 100% 80% / 0)`);
    ctx.fillStyle = glow;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

    for (const [i, star] of stars.entries()) {
      const x = ((star.x + phase * star.speed * 0.08) % 1) * width;
      const y = ((star.y - phase * star.speed * 0.04) % 1 + 1) % 1 * height;
      const fade = Math.min(1, x / 55);
      const twinkle = 0.45 + Math.sin(phase * 2 + i * 1.9) * 0.25;
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${fade * twinkle * (0.4 + ratio * 0.6)})`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(x, y, star.size * (1 + ratio * 0.5), star.size * 0.65, -0.45, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    const shade = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, Math.max(fill, 32), 0);
    shade.addColorStop(0, "rgba(36,39,44,.8)");
    shade.addColorStop(0.45, "rgba(36,39,44,0)");
    ctx.fillStyle = shade;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
  };

  const observer = new ResizeObserver(() => { resize(); draw(lastTime, true); });
  observer.observe(canvas);
  resize();
  return { draw, dispose: () => observer.disconnect() };
}


/* ===== components/ui/ListItem.tsx · SHA256 6b37ab90dc15d2c4ef87dc8b1cf457e594026d92abe9dd995824018226b9810f ===== */
import type { HTMLAttributes } from "react";

  sliding = false,

  className,
  ...rest

/* ===== components/ui/MorphingMenuShell.tsx · SHA256 70d3e7d2898bcb3e7c89b0d5258177986ae05c1b185667db11a33f88928c2963 ===== */
import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import gsap from "gsap";
import { continuousRadius, computedCornerCurvature, CORNER_CURVATURE } from "../../theme/continuousCorners";
import { cn } from "../shadcn/utils";
import { MenuTriggerOpenContext, MorphPanelLayoutContext } from "./MorphPanelLayoutContext";
import { SHARE_MOTION, motionColor, prefersReducedMotion, springEase } from "./springMotion";
import "./menuSurface.css";

type Props = {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  layoutId: string;
  trigger: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  panelClassName?: string;
  panelShadow?: string;
  panelAlign?: "left" | "right";
  panelPlacement?: "above" | "below";
  zIndex?: number;
  staggerContent?: boolean;
  unstyled?: boolean;
  triggerLayout?: "inline" | "block";
  children: ReactNode;
};

const OPEN_SHADOW = "0 6px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.10)";
const CLOSED_SHADOW = "0 0 0 rgba(0,0,0,0)";

/** The approved share demo's single shell, compact face and expanded face. */
export function MorphingMenuShell({ open, onOpenChange, layoutId, trigger, className,
  panelClassName, panelShadow = OPEN_SHADOW, panelAlign = "left", panelPlacement = "above", zIndex = 50,
  staggerContent = false, unstyled = false, triggerLayout = "inline", children,
}: Props) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const anchorRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const shellRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const compactRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const contentRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const previousOpen = useRef<boolean>();
  const latest = useRef({ open, onOpenChange });
  latest.current = { open, onOpenChange };
  const [present, setPresent] = useState(open);
  const [layoutAnimating, setLayoutAnimating] = useState(false);
  const showPanel = open || present;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const outside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (!containerRef.current?.contains(event.target as Node)) latest.current.onOpenChange(false);
    };
    const escape = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") latest.current.onOpenChange(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", outside);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", escape);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", outside);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", escape);
    };
  }, [open]);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const shell = shellRef.current, anchor = anchorRef.current, compact = compactRef.current;
    const panel = panelRef.current, content = contentRef.current;
    if (!shell || !anchor || !compact) return;
    const first = previousOpen.current === undefined;
    previousOpen.current = open;
    let disposed = false;
    let timeline: gsap.core.Timeline | undefined;
    let resize: gsap.core.Tween | undefined;
    let restoreFocus = shell.contains(document.activeElement);

    const compactSize = () => {
      if (triggerLayout === "block") compact.style.width = `${anchor.clientWidth}px`;
      const button = compact.querySelector("button");
      const css = getComputedStyle(button ?? compact);
      const width = compact.offsetWidth, height = compact.offsetHeight;
      return { width, height, radius: Math.min(parseFloat(css.borderTopLeftRadius) || height / 2, height / 2),
        curvature: computedCornerCurvature(css),
        background: motionColor(css.backgroundColor) };
    };
    let closed = compactSize();
    const restClosed = () => {
      gsap.set(compact, { opacity: 1, clearProps: "position,left,right,top,bottom,transform" });
      gsap.set(shell, { clearProps: "position,left,right,top,bottom,width,height,maxWidth,overflow,borderRadius,backgroundColor,boxShadow,transform,--morph-corner-curvature" });
      anchor.style.width = ""; anchor.style.height = "";
      if (triggerLayout === "block") compact.style.width = "100%";
    };
    compact.inert = open;
    if (panel) panel.inert = !open;
    if (!showPanel || !panel || !content) {
      restClosed();
      return;
    }
    if (open) setPresent(true);

    // Reserve the compact control's layout slot. The same shell owns both sizes.
    anchor.style.width = triggerLayout === "block" ? "100%" : `${closed.width}px`;
    anchor.style.height = `${closed.height}px`;
    if (shell.style.position !== "absolute") {
      gsap.set(shell, { position: "absolute", width: closed.width, height: closed.height,
        maxWidth: "none", borderRadius: closed.radius, "--morph-corner-curvature": closed.curvature, backgroundColor: closed.background });
    }
    gsap.set(shell, { left: panelAlign === "left" ? 0 : "auto", right: panelAlign === "right" ? 0 : "auto",
      bottom: panelPlacement === "above" ? 0 : "auto", top: panelPlacement === "below" ? 0 : "auto" });
    gsap.set(compact, { position: "absolute", left: panelAlign === "left" ? 0 : "auto",
      right: panelAlign === "right" ? 0 : "auto", bottom: panelPlacement === "above" ? 0 : "auto",
      top: panelPlacement === "below" ? 0 : "auto" });
    const expandedSize = () => {
      panel.style.width = "";
      const scrollTop = content.scrollTop;
      content.style.maxHeight = "";
      content.style.overflowY = "";
      const width = Math.min(content.offsetWidth, window.innerWidth - 32);
      panel.style.width = `${width}px`;
      const rect = anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
      const availableHeight = panelPlacement === "above" ? rect.bottom - 16 : window.innerHeight - rect.top - 16;
      if (panel.offsetHeight > availableHeight) {
        content.style.maxHeight = `${Math.max(24, availableHeight)}px`;
        content.style.overflowY = "auto";
        content.scrollTop = scrollTop;
      }
      const left = panelAlign === "right" ? rect.right - width : rect.left;
      return { width, height: panel.offsetHeight,
        x: Math.max(16, Math.min(left, window.innerWidth - width - 16)) - left };
    };
    let expanded = expandedSize();
    const finish = () => {
      if (disposed || latest.current.open !== open) return;
      setLayoutAnimating(false);
      if (open) shell.style.overflow = "visible";
      else {
        restClosed();
        setPresent(false);
      }
    };
    const focus = () => {
      if (!disposed && restoreFocus && document.activeElement === document.body && latest.current.open === open) {
        (open ? panel : compact.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>("button"))?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      }
    };
    const play = () => {
      timeline?.kill(); resize?.kill();
      const reduced = prefersReducedMotion() || (first && open);
      if (reduced) {
        gsap.set(shell, { width: open ? expanded.width : closed.width, height: open ? expanded.height : closed.height,
          x: open ? expanded.x : 0, borderRadius: open ? continuousRadius(16) : closed.radius,
          "--morph-corner-curvature": open ? CORNER_CURVATURE : closed.curvature,
          backgroundColor: open ? motionColor(getComputedStyle(panel).backgroundColor) : closed.background,
          boxShadow: open ? panelShadow : CLOSED_SHADOW });
        gsap.set(compact, { opacity: open ? 0 : 1, scale: 1 });
        gsap.set(panel, { opacity: open ? 1 : 0, filter: "blur(0px)" });
        finish(); focus();
        return;
      }
      setLayoutAnimating(true);
      shell.style.overflow = "hidden";
      const motion = open ? SHARE_MOTION.open : SHARE_MOTION.close;
      timeline = gsap.timeline({ onComplete: finish });
      timeline.to(shell, { width: open ? expanded.width : closed.width, duration: motion.width.duration,
        ease: springEase(motion.width.stiffness, motion.width.damping, motion.width.duration) }, motion.width.delay);
      timeline.to(shell, { height: open ? expanded.height : closed.height, duration: motion.height.duration,
        ease: springEase(motion.height.stiffness, motion.height.damping, motion.height.duration) }, motion.height.delay);
      timeline.to(shell, { x: open ? expanded.x : 0, borderRadius: open ? continuousRadius(16) : closed.radius,
        "--morph-corner-curvature": open ? CORNER_CURVATURE : closed.curvature,
        backgroundColor: open ? motionColor(getComputedStyle(panel).backgroundColor) : closed.background,
        boxShadow: open ? panelShadow : CLOSED_SHADOW, duration: 0.22, ease: "power2.out" }, 0);
      timeline.to(compact, { opacity: open ? 0 : 1, duration: 0.14, ease: "power2.out" }, open ? 0 : 0.2);
      if (!open) timeline.fromTo(compact, { scale: 0.95 }, { scale: 1, duration: 0.48,
        ease: springEase(300, 21, 0.48) }, 0.2);
      timeline.to(panel, { opacity: open ? 1 : 0, filter: open ? "blur(0px)" : "blur(5px)",
        duration: open ? 0.24 : 0.18, ease: "power2.out" }, open ? 0.12 : 0);
      if (open && staggerContent) {
        const rows = Array.from(content.querySelectorAll("[role=menuitem], [data-menu-item]")).slice(0, 24);
        timeline.fromTo(rows, { opacity: 0, y: 6 }, { opacity: 1, y: 0,
          duration: 0.5, stagger: Math.min(0.025, 0.16 / Math.max(1, rows.length)), ease: springEase(280, 24, 0.5) }, 0.13);
      }
      timeline.call(focus, [], 0.36);
    };
    play();
    const retarget = () => {
      if (disposed) return;
      closed = compactSize();
      if (!latest.current.open) return;
      const next = expandedSize();
      if (Math.abs(next.width - expanded.width) < 0.5 && Math.abs(next.height - expanded.height) < 0.5 && Math.abs(next.x - expanded.x) < 0.5) return;
      const widthChanged = Math.abs(next.width - expanded.width) >= 0.5;
      expanded = next;
      resize?.kill();
      gsap.killTweensOf(shell, widthChanged ? "width,height,x" : "height,x");
      const motion = SHARE_MOTION.resize;
      resize = gsap.to(shell, { ...(widthChanged ? { width: expanded.width } : {}), x: expanded.x, height: expanded.height,
        duration: prefersReducedMotion() ? 0 : motion.duration,
        ease: springEase(motion.stiffness, motion.damping, motion.duration) });
    };
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(retarget);
    observer.observe(content); observer.observe(compact);
    const outsideFocus = (event: FocusEvent) => { if (!shell.contains(event.target as Node)) restoreFocus = false; };
    const media = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    document.addEventListener("focusin", outsideFocus);
    window.addEventListener("resize", retarget);
    media.addEventListener("change", play);
    return () => {
      disposed = true; timeline?.kill(); resize?.kill(); observer.disconnect();
      document.removeEventListener("focusin", outsideFocus);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", retarget);
      media.removeEventListener("change", play);
    };
  }, [open, showPanel, panelAlign, panelPlacement, panelShadow, triggerLayout]);

  return <div ref={containerRef} className={cn("relative", className)}>
    <div ref={anchorRef} className={cn("relative max-w-full align-top", triggerLayout === "block" ? "flex w-full" : "inline-flex")}>
      <div ref={shellRef} data-motion-id={layoutId} data-state={open ? "open" : "closed"}
        data-morph-placement={panelPlacement} className={cn("morph-menu-shell relative inline-flex max-w-full", triggerLayout === "block" && "w-full")}
        style={{ zIndex: showPanel ? zIndex : undefined, pointerEvents: !open && present ? "none" : undefined }}>
        <div ref={compactRef} className={cn("morph-menu-compact pointer-events-auto inline-flex max-w-full", triggerLayout === "block" ? "w-full" : "w-max")}
          aria-hidden={open}>
          <MenuTriggerOpenContext.Provider value={open}>{trigger}</MenuTriggerOpenContext.Provider>
        </div>
        {showPanel && <div ref={panelRef} data-morph-panel aria-hidden={!open} tabIndex={-1}
          className="morph-menu-expanded ui-menu-surface absolute left-0 top-0 w-max max-w-[calc(100vw-32px)] rounded-2xl bg-elevated-bg"
          style={{ opacity: 0, pointerEvents: open ? "auto" : "none" }}>
          <MorphPanelLayoutContext.Provider value={layoutAnimating}>
            <div ref={contentRef} className={cn("morph-menu-content max-w-[calc(100vw-32px)]", !unstyled && "bg-elevated-bg", panelClassName)}>{children}</div>
          </MorphPanelLayoutContext.Provider>
        </div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>;
}
