/* ===== components/ui/MessageSendMotionContext.tsx · SHA256 ef313a23bea029b66cd0a61f09abc4e9f9dc9ae4203271b24b2ef2178f83d83d ===== */
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { MessageSendMotion } from "./messageSendMotion";
import "./messageSendMotion.css";

export const MessageSendMotionContext = createContext<MessageSendMotion | null>(null);
export const useMessageSendMotion = () => useContext(MessageSendMotionContext);

export function useMessageSendMotionController() {
  const controller = useRef<MessageSendMotion>();
  if (!controller.current) controller.current = new MessageSendMotion();
  const motion = controller.current;
  useEffect(() => () => motion.finish(), [motion]);
  return motion;
}

export function useMessageSendTarget(id: string) {
  const motion = useMessageSendMotion();
  const release = useRef<() => void>();
  // Register through the stable row. Framer Motion 11 popLayout replaces its
  // direct child's ref, so a ref placed on that motion.div is never called.
  return useCallback((row: HTMLDivElement | null) => {
    release.current?.();
    const target = row?.querySelector<HTMLElement>("[data-user-message-id]");
    release.current = target ? motion?.register(id, target) : undefined;
  }, [motion, id]);
}


/* ===== components/ui/messageSendMotion.ts · SHA256 fad6078596729a1b05cebc538b56b1ffbb6faefcf69950032d3a8745eb2ef5b0 ===== */
import { playMessageSendFlight, type SendBox, type SendFlight } from "./messageSendFlight";
import { motionColor, prefersReducedMotion } from "./springMotion";

type PendingSend = {
  text: string;
  id: string | null;
  taken: boolean;
  nextFrame: string | null;
  source: DOMRect;
  composer: HTMLElement;
  root: HTMLDivElement;
  carrier: HTMLDivElement;
  font: { fontSize: number; lineHeight: number };
  timeout: number;
  flight?: SendFlight;
  reveal?: () => void;
};

/** Visual state only; message identity comes from App's existing seeded turn.
 * Never matches a history bubble by its text or changes the send/run protocol. */
export class MessageSendMotion {
  private current: PendingSend | null = null;
  private targets = new Map<string, HTMLElement>();
  private frame: string | null = null;

  setFrame(frame: string) {
    if (this.frame === frame) return;
    this.frame = frame;
    if (this.current && this.current.nextFrame !== frame) this.finish();
  }

  prepare(editor: HTMLTextAreaElement, mirror: HTMLElement, composer: HTMLElement, text: string) {
    this.finish();
    if (prefersReducedMotion() || !text.trim()) return;
    const source = editor.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!source.width || !source.height) return;
    const style = getComputedStyle(mirror);
    const root = document.createElement("div");
    root.className = "message-send-flight-root";
    root.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
    const carrier = document.createElement("div");
    carrier.className = "message-send-carrier";
    Object.assign(carrier.style, {
      left: "0px", top: "0px", width: `${source.width}px`, height: `${source.height}px`,
      transform: `translate(${source.left}px, ${source.top}px)`, padding: "0px",
      fontFamily: style.fontFamily, fontSize: style.fontSize, lineHeight: style.lineHeight,
      fontWeight: style.fontWeight, letterSpacing: style.letterSpacing, color: style.color,
    });
    const content = mirror.cloneNode(true) as HTMLElement;
    content.removeAttribute("id");
    content.querySelectorAll(".composer-mirror-caret").forEach(node => node.remove());
    Object.assign(content.style, { position: "relative", inset: "auto", maxHeight: "none",
      minHeight: "0", height: "auto", overflow: "visible" });
    content.style.transform = `translateY(${-editor.scrollTop}px)`;
    carrier.append(content); root.append(carrier); document.body.append(root);
    const record: PendingSend = { text: text.trim(), id: null, taken: false, nextFrame: this.frame, source, composer, root, carrier,
      font: { fontSize: parseFloat(style.fontSize), lineHeight: parseFloat(style.lineHeight) },
      timeout: window.setTimeout(() => { if (this.current === record) this.finish(); }, 1800) };
    this.current = record;
  }

  /** Consume synchronously before App awaits preflight, then bind the actual id. */
  take(text: string) {
    const record = this.current;
    if (!record || record.taken || record.text !== text.trim()) return null;
    record.taken = true;
    return { bind: (id: string, nextFrame = this.frame) => {
      if (this.current !== record) return;
      record.id = id;
      record.nextFrame = nextFrame;
      // A cold, lazy-loaded ChatView may take longer than preflight. Keep the
      // source until that exact bubble mounts, without delaying the actual run.
      window.clearTimeout(record.timeout);
      record.timeout = window.setTimeout(() => { if (this.current === record) this.finish(); }, 5000);
      const target = this.targets.get(id);
      if (target) this.start(record, target);
    } };
  }

  register(id: string, target: HTMLElement) {
    this.targets.set(id, target);
    if (this.current?.id === id && !this.current.flight) this.start(this.current, target);
    return () => {
      if (this.targets.get(id) !== target) return;
      this.targets.delete(id);
      if (this.current?.id === id) this.finish();
    };
  }

  finish = () => {
    const record = this.current;
    if (!record) return;
    this.current = null;
    window.clearTimeout(record.timeout);
    record.flight?.cancel();
    record.reveal?.();
    record.root.remove();
    window.removeEventListener("resize", this.finish);
  };

  private start(record: PendingSend, target: HTMLElement) {
    const end = target.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!end.width || !end.height || end.bottom < 0 || end.top > window.innerHeight || prefersReducedMotion()) {
      this.finish(); return;
    }
    const style = getComputedStyle(target);
    const paddingX = parseFloat(style.paddingLeft), paddingY = parseFloat(style.paddingTop);
    // Framer's layout projection writes inline visibility while the textarea
    // shrinks / timeline scrolls. A scoped CSS attribute must own this handoff.
    const previouslyHidden = target.hasAttribute("data-message-send-hidden");
    record.reveal = () => { if (!previouslyHidden) target.removeAttribute("data-message-send-hidden"); };
    target.setAttribute("data-message-send-hidden", "");
    record.carrier.replaceChildren(...Array.from(target.cloneNode(true).childNodes));
    Object.assign(record.carrier.style, {
      padding: style.padding, fontFamily: style.fontFamily, fontWeight: style.fontWeight,
      letterSpacing: style.letterSpacing, color: style.color, cornerShape: style.getPropertyValue("corner-shape"),
    });
    // Inline content retains its actual chips; attachments remain in their
    // existing message attachment row, including attachment-only submissions.
    const start: SendBox = { x: record.source.left-paddingX, y: record.source.top-paddingY,
      width: record.source.width+paddingX*2, height: Math.max(40, Math.min(record.source.height+paddingY*2, end.height)) };
    window.clearTimeout(record.timeout);
    window.addEventListener("resize", this.finish);
    record.flight = playMessageSendFlight({ carrier: record.carrier, composer: record.composer, start,
      getEnd: () => {
        if (!target.isConnected) return null;
        const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
        const scroll = target.closest<HTMLElement>("[data-chat-scroll]");
        const viewport = scroll?.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (viewport && (rect.bottom < viewport.top || rect.top > viewport.bottom)) return null;
        return { x: rect.left, y: rect.top, width: rect.width, height: rect.height };
      },
      appearance: { start: record.font, end: {
        fontSize: parseFloat(style.fontSize), lineHeight: parseFloat(style.lineHeight),
        background: motionColor(style.backgroundColor),
        radii: [style.borderTopLeftRadius, style.borderTopRightRadius, style.borderBottomRightRadius, style.borderBottomLeftRadius].map(value => parseFloat(value)),
      } }, onFinish: () => { if (this.current === record) this.finish(); },
    });
  }
}


/* ===== components/ui/messageSendFlight.ts · SHA256 60efe4b61ca0f8e62837cb2e23c37c0da60017985982a407f00d44360b454a79 ===== */
import gsap from "gsap";
import { continuousRadius } from "../../theme/continuousCorners";
import { SHARE_MOTION, springEase } from "./springMotion";

export type SendBox = { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number };
export type SendFlight = { finish: () => void; cancel: () => void };
type Appearance = { fontSize: number; lineHeight: number; radii: number[]; background: string };
const mix = (a: number, b: number, p: number) => a + (b - a) * p;
const clamp = (p: number) => Math.max(0, Math.min(1, p));

/** The approved F preview: gentle preload, the model menu's two spring axes,
 * and one small arrival overshoot. Preview and production share this timeline. */
export function playMessageSendFlight({
  carrier, composer, start, getEnd, appearance, boundsWidth = window.innerWidth, onFinish,
}: {
  carrier: HTMLElement;
  composer: HTMLElement;
  start: SendBox;
  getEnd: () => SendBox | null;
  appearance: { start?: Partial<Appearance>; end: Appearance };
  boundsWidth?: number;
  onFinish: () => void;
}): SendFlight & { timeline: gsap.core.Timeline } {
  const progress = { width: 0, height: 0, squash: 1 };
  const axes = SHARE_MOTION.open;
  const from = { fontSize: appearance.end.fontSize, lineHeight: appearance.end.lineHeight,
    radii: Array(4).fill(continuousRadius(9)) as number[], ...appearance.start };
  const background = gsap.utils.interpolate("rgba(239,241,244,0)", appearance.end.background);
  const originalTransition = composer.style.transition;
  const originalTransform = composer.style.transform;
  const originalOrigin = composer.style.transformOrigin;
  let finished = false;
  const finish = () => {
    if (finished) return;
    finished = true;
    timeline.kill();
    gsap.set(composer, { clearProps: "transform,transformOrigin" });
    composer.style.transform = originalTransform;
    composer.style.transformOrigin = originalOrigin;
    composer.style.transition = originalTransition;
    onFinish();
  };
  const paint = () => {
    const end = getEnd();
    if (!end) { finish(); return; }
    const p = progress.height, bounded = clamp(p), widthP = progress.width;
    const width = mix(start.width, end.width, clamp(widthP));
    const height = mix(start.height, end.height, bounded);
    const centerX = mix(start.x + start.width/2, end.x + end.width/2, widthP) + 8 * Math.sin(Math.PI * bounded);
    const centerY = mix(start.y + start.height/2, end.y + end.height/2, p);
    // Compress the settled layout rather than rewrapping its last character
    // into a clipped extra line when the spring passes the final width.
    const springX = Math.max(24, mix(start.width, end.width, widthP)) / width;
    const springY = Math.max(24, mix(start.height, end.height, p)) / height;
    const lag = Math.max(-.15, Math.min(.4, widthP - p));
    const arrival = Math.max(0, p - 1);
    const radii = from.radii.map((radius, index) => mix(radius, appearance.end.radii[index], bounded));
    gsap.set(carrier, {
      x: Math.max(3, Math.min(centerX - width/2, boundsWidth - width - 3)),
      y: centerY - height/2, width, height,
      scaleX: springX * (1 + (1-progress.squash)*.15 - lag*.035 + arrival*.45),
      scaleY: springY * progress.squash * (1 + lag*.07 - arrival*.3),
      borderRadius: radii.map(radius => `${radius}px`).join(" "),
      backgroundColor: background(clamp(widthP * 5)),
      fontSize: mix(from.fontSize, appearance.end.fontSize, bounded),
      lineHeight: `${mix(from.lineHeight, appearance.end.lineHeight, bounded)}px`,
    });
  };
  const timeline = gsap.timeline({ onComplete: finish });
  composer.style.transition = "none";
  paint();
  timeline.to(composer, { scaleY: .975, y: 1.5, duration: .09, transformOrigin: "50% 100%" }, 0);
  timeline.to(composer, { scaleY: 1, y: 0, duration: axes.height.duration,
    ease: springEase(axes.height.stiffness, axes.height.damping, axes.height.duration) }, .09);
  timeline.to(progress, { squash: .94, duration: .09, ease: "power2.in", onUpdate: paint }, 0);
  timeline.to(progress, { squash: 1, duration: .22, ease: "power2.out", onUpdate: paint }, .09);
  for (const axis of ["width", "height"] as const) {
    const motion = axes[axis];
    timeline.to(progress, { [axis]: 1, duration: motion.duration,
      ease: springEase(motion.stiffness, motion.damping, motion.duration), onUpdate: paint }, .08 + motion.delay);
  }
  return { timeline, finish: () => { timeline.progress(1); finish(); }, cancel: finish };
}

/* ===== components/ComposerAttachmentList.tsx · SHA256 f87eec74ef38ca5db99d1e3b8c0f884fa29bf701390248e29c36ff99675e803f ===== */
import { FontAwesomeIcon } from "@fortawesome/react-fontawesome";
import { CircleAlert, Image, LoaderCircle, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import gsap from "gsap";
import type { ComposerAttachment } from "../types";
import { ImagePreviewTrigger } from "./ImagePreviewTrigger";
import { Button } from "./shadcn/button";
import { cn } from "./shadcn/utils";
import { HoverInfo } from "./ui/HoverInfo";
import { IconButton } from "./ui/IconButton";
import { SpringDisclosure } from "./ui/SpringDisclosure";
import { prefersReducedMotion, springEase } from "./ui/springMotion";

function AttachmentMotionItem({ exiting, onExited, children }: {
  exiting: boolean; onExited: () => void; children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const latestExit = useRef(onExited);
  latestExit.current = onExited;
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const node = ref.current;
    if (!node) return;
    const reduced = prefersReducedMotion();
    node.inert = exiting;
    const tween = exiting
      ? gsap.to(node, { opacity: 0, scale: 0.95, duration: reduced ? 0 : 0.16,
          onComplete: () => latestExit.current() })
      : gsap.fromTo(node, { opacity: 0, y: 6, scale: 0.96 }, { opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1,
          duration: reduced ? 0 : 0.42, ease: springEase(360, 24, 0.42) });
    return () => { tween.kill(); };
  }, [exiting]);
  return <div ref={ref} className="shrink-0">{children}</div>;
}

interface ComposerAttachmentListProps {
  attachments: ComposerAttachment[];
  onRemove: (id: string) => void;
}

export function ComposerAttachmentList({ attachments, onRemove }: ComposerAttachmentListProps) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [retained, setRetained] = useState(attachments);
  const activeIds = new Set(attachments.map((item) => item.id));
  const shown = [...attachments, ...retained.filter((item) => !activeIds.has(item.id))];
  useEffect(() => {
    setRetained((previous) => [...attachments, ...previous.filter((item) => !attachments.some((active) => active.id === item.id))]);
  }, [attachments]);
  const removeExited = (id: string) => setRetained((items) => items.filter((item) => item.id !== id));

  return (
    <SpringDisclosure open={shown.length > 0} unmountOnExit animateOnMount>
    <div className="-mx-1 mb-2 flex max-w-full items-start gap-2.5 overflow-x-auto px-1 pb-1 pt-1.5">
      {shown.map((item) => {
        if (item.kind === "image") {
          const failed = item.status === "error";
          const status = failed
            ? t("composer.attachments.failed")
            : item.status === "processing"
            ? t("composer.attachments.recognizing")
            : null;
          const description = status
            ? t("composer.attachments.statusLabel", {
                name: item.name,
                status: failed ? item.error ?? status : status,
              })
            : item.name;

          return (
            <AttachmentMotionItem key={item.id} exiting={!activeIds.has(item.id)} onExited={() => removeExited(item.id)}><HoverInfo
              key={item.id}
              content={description}
            >
              <div className="group/attachment relative flex h-[68px] w-[68px] shrink-0">
                {item.dataUrl ? (
                  <ImagePreviewTrigger
                    src={item.dataUrl}
                    alt={item.name}
                    triggerClassName="h-full w-full rounded-[10px] border border-border-theme bg-ui-tint"
                    imageClassName="h-full w-full object-cover"
                  />
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center rounded-[10px] border border-border-theme bg-ui-tint text-text-secondary">
                    <Image aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5" />
                  </div>
                )}
                {status && (
                  <span
                    role={failed ? "alert" : "status"}
                    aria-label={description}
                    className={cn(
                      "pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-1 bottom-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-0.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md bg-elevated-bg px-0.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] leading-4",
                      failed ? "text-red-500" : "text-text-secondary",
                    )}
                  >
                    {failed ? (
                      <CircleAlert aria-hidden="true" className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0" />
                    ) : (
                      <LoaderCircle aria-hidden="true" className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0 motion-safe:animate-spin" />
                    )}
                    <span className="min-w-0 truncate">{status}</span>
                  </span>
                )}
                <Button
                  variant="ghost"
                  size="icon"
                  aria-label={t("composer.attachments.removeImage", { name: item.name })}
                  onClick={() => onRemove(item.id)}
                  className="absolute -right-1 -top-1 z-10 h-5 w-5 rounded-full border-2 border-elevated-bg bg-neutral-600 p-0 text-white opacity-0 transition-opacity hover:bg-neutral-800 hover:text-white group-hover/attachment:opacity-100 group-focus-within/attachment:opacity-100 motion-reduce:transition-none [@media(hover:none)]:opacity-100"
                >
                  <X aria-hidden="true" className="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
                </Button>
              </div>
            </HoverInfo></AttachmentMotionItem>
          );
        }

        return (
          <AttachmentMotionItem key={item.id} exiting={!activeIds.has(item.id)} onExited={() => removeExited(item.id)}><div
            key={item.id}
            className="flex h-14 max-w-[220px] shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-black/5 px-2 text-left"
          >
            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white text-text-secondary">
              <FontAwesomeIcon icon={["fas", item.kind === "text" ? "file-lines" : "file"]} />
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="truncate text-[12px] font-medium text-text-base">{item.name}</div>
              <div className="truncate text-[11px] text-text-secondary">
                {item.status === "processing"
                  ? t("composer.attachments.processing")
                  : item.status === "error"
                  ? item.error ?? t("composer.attachments.failed")
                  : item.kind === "text"
                  ? t("composer.attachments.text")
                  : t("composer.attachments.file")}
              </div>
            </div>
            <IconButton
              type="button"
              aria-label={t("composer.attachments.removeAttachment", { name: item.name })}
              onClick={() => onRemove(item.id)}
              className="h-6 w-6 rounded-full"
            >
              <FontAwesomeIcon icon={["fas", "xmark"]} className="text-[11px]" />
            </IconButton>
          </div></AttachmentMotionItem>
        );
      })}
    </div>
    </SpringDisclosure>
  );
}

/* ===== components/shadcn/dialog.tsx · SHA256 861e4cb12da5ee2db664e2f1d920e7c46d24aaf542be798c578698317c8e2ce6 ===== */
import * as React from "react";

import {
  GlobalModal,
  GlobalModalDescription,
  GlobalModalFooter,
  GlobalModalHeader,
  GlobalModalTitle,
  modalOriginFromElement,
  type ModalTriggerOrigin,
} from "../ui/GlobalModal";
import { MOTION } from "../ui/motion";
import { cn } from "./utils";

interface DialogContextValue {
  open: boolean;
  setOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  origin: ModalTriggerOrigin | null;
  setOrigin: (origin: ModalTriggerOrigin | null) => void;
}

const DialogContext = React.createContext<DialogContextValue | null>(null);

function useDialogContext() {
  const context = React.useContext(DialogContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("Dialog components must be used inside Dialog");
  }
  return context;
}

export interface DialogProps {
  open?: boolean;
  defaultOpen?: boolean;
  onOpenChange?: (open: boolean) => void;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

function Dialog({ open, defaultOpen = false, onOpenChange, children }: DialogProps) {
  const [internalOpen, setInternalOpen] = React.useState(defaultOpen);
  const [origin, setOrigin] = React.useState<ModalTriggerOrigin | null>(null);
  const controlled = typeof open === "boolean";
  const actualOpen = controlled ? open : internalOpen;
  const setOpen = React.useCallback(
    (next: boolean) => {
      if (!controlled) setInternalOpen(next);
      onOpenChange?.(next);
    },
    [controlled, onOpenChange],
  );

  return (
    <DialogContext.Provider value={{ open: actualOpen, setOpen, origin, setOrigin }}>
      {children}
    </DialogContext.Provider>
  );
}

interface DialogTriggerProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  asChild?: boolean;
}

const DialogTrigger = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, DialogTriggerProps>(
  ({ asChild: _asChild, onClick, ...props }, ref) => {
    const { setOpen, setOrigin } = useDialogContext();
    return (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        type="button"
        onClick={(event) => {
          onClick?.(event);
          if (!event.defaultPrevented) {
            setOrigin(modalOriginFromElement(event.currentTarget));
            setOpen(true);
          }
        }}
        {...props}
      />
    );
  },
);
DialogTrigger.displayName = "DialogTrigger";

/** @deprecated Prefer GlobalModal overlay; kept for API compatibility. */
const DialogOverlay = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>>(
  ({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
    <div ref={ref} className={cn("hidden", className)} {...props} />
  ),
);
DialogOverlay.displayName = "DialogOverlay";

interface DialogContentProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> {
  /** Override the global modal shell layer for surfaces that must cover canvas overlays. */
  zIndexClass?: string;
  /** Optional overlay styling for focused viewers such as image preview. */
  backdropClassName?: string;
}

const DialogContent = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, DialogContentProps>(
  ({ className, children, zIndexClass, backdropClassName, ...props }, ref) => {
    const { open, setOpen, origin } = useDialogContext();

    return (
      <GlobalModal
        open={open}
        origin={origin}
        onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
        zIndexClass={zIndexClass}
        backdropClassName={backdropClassName}
        panelClassName={className}
        panelProps={{ role: "dialog", "aria-modal": true, ...props }}
        panelRef={ref}
      >
        {children}
      </GlobalModal>
    );
  },
);
DialogContent.displayName = "DialogContent";

const DialogHeader = ({ className, ...props }: React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) => (
  <GlobalModalHeader className={className} {...props} />
);
DialogHeader.displayName = "DialogHeader";

const DialogFooter = ({ className, ...props }: React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) => (
  <GlobalModalFooter className={className} {...props} />
);
DialogFooter.displayName = "DialogFooter";

const DialogTitle = React.forwardRef<HTMLHeadingElement, React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLHeadingElement>>(
  ({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
    <GlobalModalTitle ref={ref} className={className} {...props} />
  ),
);
DialogTitle.displayName = "DialogTitle";

const DialogDescription = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLParagraphElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLParagraphElement>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <GlobalModalDescription ref={ref} className={className} {...props} />
));
DialogDescription.displayName = "DialogDescription";

interface DialogCloseProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  asChild?: boolean;
}

const DialogClose = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, DialogCloseProps>(
  ({ asChild: _asChild, onClick, ...props }, ref) => {
    const { setOpen } = useDialogContext();
    return (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        type="button"
        onClick={(event) => {
          onClick?.(event);
          if (!event.defaultPrevented) setOpen(false);
        }}
        {...props}
      />
    );
  },
);
DialogClose.displayName = "DialogClose";

/** 弹窗右上角关闭 —— 6px 圆角 tint 底（非正圆 pill） */
const DialogCloseIcon = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, DialogCloseProps>(
  ({ className, children, onClick, ...props }, ref) => {
    const { setOpen } = useDialogContext();
    return (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        type="button"
        className={cn(
          "flex h-8 w-8 flex-shrink-0 appearance-none items-center justify-center rounded-md border-0 bg-transparent text-text-secondary",
          MOTION.fast,
          "hover:bg-ui-tint-strong hover:text-text-base active:bg-ui-tint-strong",
          className,
        )}
        onClick={(event) => {
          onClick?.(event);
          if (!event.defaultPrevented) setOpen(false);
        }}
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
      </button>
    );
  },
);
DialogCloseIcon.displayName = "DialogCloseIcon";

export {
  Dialog,
  DialogClose,
  DialogCloseIcon,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogOverlay,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogTrigger,
};


/* ===== components/ui/GlobalModal.tsx · SHA256 888a99dd9a45b6693e5e67368eee16fcb31f49c9df34869a67b61717eb72a37e ===== */
import {
  forwardRef,
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useLayoutEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type HTMLAttributes,
  type MouseEvent,
  type MutableRefObject,
  type ReactNode,
  type Ref,
} from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";

import { cn } from "../shadcn/utils";
import {
  playModalOriginClose,
  playModalOriginOpen,
  prefersReducedMotion,
  type ModalTriggerOrigin,
} from "./modalFromOriginMotion";
import { Panel } from "./Panel";

export type { ModalTriggerOrigin } from "./modalFromOriginMotion";
export { modalOriginFromElement } from "./modalFromOriginMotion";

export interface GlobalModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  children: ReactNode;
  /** 触发按钮中心；方案 A 从该点 scale 展开/收回 */
  origin?: ModalTriggerOrigin | null;
  className?: string;
  panelClassName?: string;
  panelProps?: Omit<HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>, "children" | "className">;
  panelRef?: Ref<HTMLDivElement>;
  backdropClassName?: string;
  closeOnBackdrop?: boolean;
  closeOnEscape?: boolean;
  zIndexClass?: string;
}

/**
 * 全局居中弹窗 —— portal + GSAP 从触发原点 scale 展开/收回（方案 A）。
 */
export function GlobalModal({
  open,
  onClose,
  children,
  origin = null,
  className,
  panelClassName,
  panelProps,
  panelRef,
  backdropClassName,
  closeOnBackdrop = true,
  closeOnEscape = true,
  zIndexClass = "z-[200]",
}: GlobalModalProps) {
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(open);
  const backdropRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const panelElementRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const originRef = useRef<ModalTriggerOrigin | null>(origin);
  const tlRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof playModalOriginOpen> | null>(null);
  const closingRef = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open && origin) {
      originRef.current = origin;
    }
  }, [open, origin]);

  const finishClose = useCallback(() => {
    closingRef.current = false;
    setMounted(false);
    originRef.current = null;
    onClose();
  }, [onClose]);

  const beginClose = useCallback(() => {
    if (closingRef.current || !mounted) return;

    const backdrop = backdropRef.current;
    const panel = panelElementRef.current;
    if (!backdrop || !panel) {
      finishClose();
      return;
    }

    closingRef.current = true;
    tlRef.current?.kill();
    if (prefersReducedMotion()) {
      finishClose();
      return;
    }
    tlRef.current = playModalOriginClose(backdrop, panel, originRef.current);
    tlRef.current.eventCallback("onComplete", finishClose);
  }, [finishClose, mounted]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      setMounted(true);
      closingRef.current = false;
      return;
    }
    if (mounted && !closingRef.current) {
      beginClose();
    }
  }, [beginClose, mounted, open]);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted || !open || closingRef.current) return;

    const backdrop = backdropRef.current;
    const panel = panelElementRef.current;
    if (!backdrop || !panel) return;

    tlRef.current?.kill();
    tlRef.current = playModalOriginOpen(backdrop, panel, originRef.current);

    return () => {
      tlRef.current?.kill();
    };
  }, [mounted, open]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted || !closeOnEscape) return;
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") beginClose();
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, [beginClose, closeOnEscape, mounted]);

  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      tlRef.current?.kill();
    },
    [],
  );

  if (!mounted || typeof document === "undefined") return null;

  const handleShellMouseDown = (event: MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (!closeOnBackdrop || closingRef.current) return;
    if (event.target === event.currentTarget || event.target === backdropRef.current) {
      beginClose();
    }
  };

  const { onMouseDown: onPanelMouseDown, ...restPanelProps } = panelProps ?? {};

  const setPanelRef = (element: HTMLDivElement | null) => {
    panelElementRef.current = element;
    if (typeof panelRef === "function") {
      panelRef(element);
    } else if (panelRef) {
      (panelRef as MutableRefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>).current = element;
    }
  };

  return createPortal(
    <div
      className={cn("fixed inset-0 flex items-center justify-center px-4", zIndexClass, className)}
      onMouseDown={handleShellMouseDown}
    >
      <div
        ref={backdropRef}
        className={cn("absolute inset-0 bg-black/20", backdropClassName)}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />
      <Panel
        ref={setPanelRef}
        menu={false}
        {...restPanelProps}
        className={cn(
          "relative flex max-h-[88vh] w-full max-w-[620px] flex-col overflow-hidden",
          panelClassName,
        )}
        onMouseDown={(event) => {
          event.stopPropagation();
          onPanelMouseDown?.(event);
        }}
      >
        {children}
      </Panel>
    </div>,
    document.body,
  );
}

export function GlobalModalHeader({ className, ...props }: HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) {
  return <div className={cn("flex items-start justify-between gap-4 px-6 py-5", className)} {...props} />;
}

export function GlobalModalFooter({ className, ...props }: HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) {
  return <div className={cn("flex justify-end gap-2 px-6 py-4", className)} {...props} />;
}

export const GlobalModalTitle = forwardRef<HTMLHeadingElement, HTMLAttributes<HTMLHeadingElement>>(
  function GlobalModalTitle({ className, ...props }, ref) {
    return <h2 ref={ref} className={cn("text-xl font-semibold text-text-base", className)} {...props} />;
  },
);

export const GlobalModalDescription = forwardRef<
  HTMLParagraphElement,
  HTMLAttributes<HTMLParagraphElement>
>(function GlobalModalDescription({ className, ...props }, ref) {
  return <p ref={ref} className={cn("mt-1 text-[13px] leading-5 text-text-secondary", className)} {...props} />;
});

export function GlobalModalBody({ className, ...props }: HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) {
  return <div className={cn("px-6 py-6", className)} {...props} />;
}


/* ===== components/ui/modalFromOriginMotion.ts · SHA256 639524f0669815c8152b087a36e193c09fff58c70f73ae9754049b7943247070 ===== */
import gsap from "gsap";
import { prefersReducedMotion } from "./springMotion";
export { prefersReducedMotion } from "./springMotion";

/** 方案 A：从触发按钮中心 scale 展开 / 收回 */
export const MODAL_ORIGIN_MOTION = {
  openBackdropDuration: 0.22,
  openPanelDuration: 0.32,
  openPanelDelay: 0.02,
  closePanelDuration: 0.2,
  closeBackdropDuration: 0.18,
  closeBackdropDelay: 0.02,
  scaleFrom: 0.96,
  openPanelEase: "power3.out",
  openBackdropEase: "power2.out",
  closePanelEase: "power2.inOut",
  closeBackdropEase: "power2.in",
} as const;

export interface ModalTriggerOrigin {
  centerX: number;
  centerY: number;
}

export function originTransformFromPanel(panel: HTMLElement, origin: ModalTriggerOrigin): string {
  const rect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
  const originX = origin.centerX - rect.left;
  const originY = origin.centerY - rect.top;
  return `${originX}px ${originY}px`;
}

export function modalOriginFromElement(element: HTMLElement): ModalTriggerOrigin {
  const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
  return {
    centerX: rect.left + rect.width / 2,
    centerY: rect.top + rect.height / 2,
  };
}

export function playModalOriginOpen(
  backdrop: HTMLElement,
  panel: HTMLElement,
  origin: ModalTriggerOrigin | null,
): gsap.core.Timeline {
  const tl = gsap.timeline();

  if (prefersReducedMotion()) {
    gsap.set(backdrop, { autoAlpha: 1 });
    gsap.set(panel, { autoAlpha: 1, scale: 1, clearProps: "transform,transformOrigin" });
    return tl;
  }

  const transformOrigin = origin ? originTransformFromPanel(panel, origin) : "center center";
  gsap.set(backdrop, { autoAlpha: 0 });
  gsap.set(panel, {
    opacity: 0,
    visibility: "visible",
    scale: MODAL_ORIGIN_MOTION.scaleFrom,
    transformOrigin,
  });

  tl.to(backdrop, {
    autoAlpha: 1,
    duration: MODAL_ORIGIN_MOTION.openBackdropDuration,
    ease: MODAL_ORIGIN_MOTION.openBackdropEase,
  }, 0).to(
    panel,
    {
      opacity: 1,
      scale: 1,
      duration: MODAL_ORIGIN_MOTION.openPanelDuration,
      ease: MODAL_ORIGIN_MOTION.openPanelEase,
    },
    MODAL_ORIGIN_MOTION.openPanelDelay,
  );

  return tl;
}

export function playModalOriginClose(
  backdrop: HTMLElement,
  panel: HTMLElement,
  origin: ModalTriggerOrigin | null,
): gsap.core.Timeline {
  const tl = gsap.timeline();

  if (prefersReducedMotion()) {
    gsap.set([backdrop, panel], { autoAlpha: 0 });
    return tl;
  }

  const transformOrigin = origin ? originTransformFromPanel(panel, origin) : "center center";
  gsap.set(panel, { transformOrigin });

  tl.to(
    panel,
    {
      autoAlpha: 0,
      scale: MODAL_ORIGIN_MOTION.scaleFrom,
      duration: MODAL_ORIGIN_MOTION.closePanelDuration,
      ease: MODAL_ORIGIN_MOTION.closePanelEase,
    },
    0,
  ).to(
    backdrop,
    {
      autoAlpha: 0,
      duration: MODAL_ORIGIN_MOTION.closeBackdropDuration,
      ease: MODAL_ORIGIN_MOTION.closeBackdropEase,
    },
    MODAL_ORIGIN_MOTION.closeBackdropDelay,
  );

  return tl;
}


/* ===== components/ui/IconButton.tsx · SHA256 8441e89fb66d94d047d0529737da2999e82755c93d12d8b5d271c5531aaff117 ===== */
import { forwardRef, type ButtonHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../shadcn/utils";
import { MOTION } from "./motion";
import { useSpringPress } from "./useSpringPress";

/**
 * 图标按钮 —— 统一语言：无边框、透明底、hover 静默着色。
 * 用于工具栏、行内操作等小图标按钮。

      <button
        ref={pressRef}
        className={cn(
          "ui-circle-icon-btn flex h-7 w-7 flex-shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-text-secondary",
          MOTION.fast,
          "hover:bg-ui-tint hover:text-text-base",
          "disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40 disabled:hover:bg-transparent",
          className,

        {...rest}
      />
    );
  },

/* ===== components/ui/SpringDisclosure.tsx · SHA256 e3428445ed16330bbfc1294dcb3fc3ab6c0f5736efbc7bfba2c4df454408c269 ===== */
import { useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import gsap from "gsap";
import { SOFT_SPRING, prefersReducedMotion, springEase } from "./springMotion";
import { cn } from "../shadcn/utils";

/** Layout-size animation shared by process details, attachments and sidebars. */
export function SpringDisclosure({ open, children, className, axis = "height", size,
  unmountOnExit = false, animateOnMount = false,
}: { open: boolean; children: ReactNode; className?: string; axis?: "height" | "width";
  size?: number; unmountOnExit?: boolean; animateOnMount?: boolean }) {
  const shellRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const bodyRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const lastSize = useRef(0);
  const initialized = useRef(false);
  const tweenRef = useRef<gsap.core.Timeline>();
  const [present, setPresent] = useState(open);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const first = !initialized.current;
    initialized.current = true;
    const shell = shellRef.current, body = bodyRef.current;
    if (!shell || !body) return;
    if (open) setPresent(true);
    body.inert = !open;
    let disposed = false;
    const animate = () => {
      const natural = axis === "width" ? size ?? body.offsetWidth : body.offsetHeight;
      const target = open ? natural : 0;
      if (shell.style[axis] === "auto" && !open) gsap.set(shell, { [axis]: lastSize.current });
      lastSize.current = natural || lastSize.current;
      tweenRef.current?.kill();
      const reduced = prefersReducedMotion() || (first && !animateOnMount);
      const m = SOFT_SPRING;
      tweenRef.current = gsap.timeline({ onComplete: () => {
        if (disposed) return;
        if (open && axis === "height") shell.style.height = "auto";
        if (!open && unmountOnExit) setPresent(false);
      } });
      tweenRef.current.to(shell, { [axis]: target, duration: reduced ? 0 : m.duration,
        ease: springEase(m.stiffness, m.damping, m.duration) }, 0);
      tweenRef.current.to(body, { opacity: open ? 1 : 0, y: open ? 0 : -3,
        filter: open ? "blur(0px)" : "blur(2px)", duration: reduced ? 0 : open ? 0.24 : 0.14,
        ease: "power2.out" }, 0);
    };
    animate();
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(() => {
      const next = axis === "width" ? size ?? body.offsetWidth : body.offsetHeight;
      if (open && Math.abs(next - lastSize.current) > 0.5) animate();
    });
    observer.observe(body);
    const media = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
    media.addEventListener("change", animate);
    return () => { disposed = true; observer.disconnect(); media.removeEventListener("change", animate); tweenRef.current?.kill(); };
  }, [open, axis, size, unmountOnExit, animateOnMount]);

  if (unmountOnExit && !open && !present) return null;
  return <div ref={shellRef} data-spring-disclosure={axis} aria-hidden={!open}
    className={cn("overflow-hidden", axis === "width" && "h-full shrink-0", className)} style={{ [axis]: 0 }}>
    <div ref={bodyRef} className={axis === "height" ? "flow-root" : "h-full"}
      style={axis === "width" ? { width: size } : undefined}>{children}</div>
  </div>;
}


/* ===== components/message.tsx · SHA256 6c10339bf3215e88d096b2e11d72d599246a99382f54da30582c33a308a90246 ===== */
import { createRoot, type Root } from "react-dom/client";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { FontAwesomeIcon } from "@fortawesome/react-fontawesome";
import type { IconProp } from "@fortawesome/fontawesome-svg-core";

// A dependency-free, imperative toast/message API (antd-style):
//   message.error("..."), message.success("..."), message.info("..."),
//   message.warning("...").
// It lazily mounts a single fixed container at the top-center of the window and
// renders an animated stack of auto-dismissing notices.

type MessageType = "success" | "error" | "info" | "warning";

interface Notice {
  id: number;
  type: MessageType;
  text: string;
  duration: number;
}

const TYPE_META: Record<MessageType, { icon: IconProp; color: string }> = {
  success: { icon: ["fas", "circle-check"], color: "text-green-500" },
  error: { icon: ["fas", "circle-info"], color: "text-red-500" },
  info: { icon: ["fas", "circle-info"], color: "text-blue-500" },
  warning: { icon: ["fas", "circle-info"], color: "text-amber-500" },
};

// Module-level bridge so the imperative API can push into the mounted host.
let pushNotice: ((n: Omit<Notice, "id">) => void) | null = null;
let seq = 0;
let root: Root | null = null;

function ensureHost() {
  if (root) return;
  const el = document.createElement("div");
  el.id = "message-root";
  document.body.appendChild(el);
  root = createRoot(el);
  root.render(<MessageHost />);
}

function MessageHost() {
  const [notices, setNotices] = useState<Notice[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    pushNotice = (n) => {
      const id = ++seq;
      setNotices((prev) => [...prev, { ...n, id }]);
      window.setTimeout(() => {
        setNotices((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x.id !== id));
      }, n.duration);
    };
    return () => {
      pushNotice = null;
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="fixed top-4 inset-x-0 z-[9999] flex flex-col items-center gap-2 pointer-events-none">
      {notices.map((n) => {
        const meta = TYPE_META[n.type];
        return (
          <div
            key={n.id}
            className="message-notice pointer-events-auto flex items-center gap-2.5 bg-white border border-border-theme rounded-lg shadow-[0_6px_24px_rgb(0,0,0,0.12)] px-4 py-2.5 max-w-[480px]"
          >
            <FontAwesomeIcon icon={meta.icon} className={`${meta.color} text-sm`} />
            <span className="text-[13px] text-text-base">{n.text}</span>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

function show(type: MessageType, text: string, duration = 3000) {
  ensureHost();
  // Defer one tick so a freshly-created host has registered its pusher.
  if (pushNotice) {
    pushNotice({ type, text, duration });
  } else {
    setTimeout(() => pushNotice?.({ type, text, duration }), 0);
  }
}

export const message = {
  success: (text: string, duration?: number) => show("success", text, duration),
  error: (text: string, duration?: number) => show("error", text, duration),
  info: (text: string, duration?: number) => show("info", text, duration),
  warning: (text: string, duration?: number) => show("warning", text, duration),
};

/** A live tool-call card shown inline in an assistant message. */
export interface ToolCall {

/** One ordered segment of an assistant turn, in the order it streamed in. */
export type MessagePart =
  | { kind: "reasoning"; text: string }
  | { kind: "text"; text: string; tone?: "normal" | "error" }
  | { kind: "tool"; tool: ToolCall }

    }

/** A reconstructed conversation part from a replayed session (mirrors
 * deepagent-app-core::ConversationPartDto). */
export type ConversationPart =
  | { kind: "text"; text: string }
  | { kind: "reasoning"; text: string }
  | {
      kind: "compact_boundary";
      tokens_before: number;
      tokens_after: number;
      strategy: string;

    }
  | {
      kind: "memories";

      summary?: string | null;
      meta?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
    };

/** A reconstructed conversation message (mirrors ConversationMessageDto).
 * `system` marks a session-level event row (compaction boundary, injected
 * memories) rather than something either side said. */
export interface ConversationMessage {
  id?: string;
  turn?: { version: 1; id: string; terminal_kind?: string | null; final_answer?: string | null };
  role: "user" | "assistant" | "system";
  content: string;
  attachments?: PersistedAttachment[];
  parts: ConversationPart[];
  usage?: ConversationUsage;
}

/** Persisted per-turn usage (mirrors ConversationUsageDto). */
export interface ConversationUsage {
  prompt_tokens: number;
  completion_tokens: number;
  reasoning_tokens: number;
  total_tokens: number;
  prompt_cache_hit_tokens: number;
  prompt_cache_miss_tokens: number;
  duration_ms: number;
  raw_responses_usage?: unknown;
  cost_yuan?: number;
}

/**
 * Token usage for THIS assistant turn (per-turn, not cumulative).
 *
 * For stateless models like DeepSeek, every request resends the full context:
 * - `promptTokens`: current context window size (grows until compaction)
 * - `completionTokens`: THIS turn's output tokens (NOT cumulative)
 * - `totalTokens`: should equal `promptTokens + completionTokens`
 *
 * For cumulative session-wide statistics, see `CostSummary` (billing ledger).
 * For current window occupancy, see `ContextUsageSnapshot.projected_request_tokens`.
 */
export interface TokenUsage {
  /** Prompt (input) tokens from THIS turn (current context window size). */
  promptTokens: number;
  /** Completion (output) tokens from THIS turn (NOT cumulative). */
  completionTokens: number;
  /** Reasoning tokens from THIS turn (subset of completionTokens). */
  reasoningTokens: number;
  /** Total tokens for THIS turn (should equal promptTokens + completionTokens). */
  totalTokens: number;
  /** Cache hit tokens from THIS turn (last-wins). */
  cacheHitTokens: number;
  /** Cache miss tokens from THIS turn (last-wins). */
  cacheMissTokens: number;
  /** Cost in CNY/RMB for THIS turn. undefined when pricing unavailable. */
  costYuan?: number;
}

/** Where a resolved model capability came from (mirrors CapabilitySource). */
export type CapabilitySource =
  | "provider_metadata"
  | "bundled_official_snapshot"
  | "user_override"
  | "conservative_fallback";

/**
 * Resolved capability for one model (mirrors deepagent-models::ModelCapability).
 * Resolved by the backend through the same resolver the runtime uses, so the UI
 * shows the real context capacity instead of guessing it from the model id.
 */
export interface ModelCapability {
  model_id: string;
  context_window: number;
  max_output_tokens: number;
  supports_tools: boolean;
  supports_thinking: boolean;
  supports_json_output: boolean;
  capability_source: CapabilitySource;
  fallback_reason?: string;
}

export type ContextBlockKind =
  | "stable_prefix"
  | "dynamic_runtime"
  | "task_summary"
  | "recent_conversation"
  | "retrieved_knowledge"
  | "retrieved_code"
  | "tool_schemas"
  | "tool_result_refs"
  | "attachments"
  | "user_goal"
  | "other";

export type ContextCacheScope = "stable_prefix" | "dynamic";

export interface ContextBlockUsage {
  name: string;
  kind: ContextBlockKind;
  tokens: number;
  cache_scope: ContextCacheScope;
  source: string;
}

export interface ContextUsageSnapshot {
  model_id: string;
  context_window: number;
  prompt_budget: number;
  /** Assembly estimate by the local counter, block by block. */
  estimated_prompt_tokens: number;
  /** Legacy alias for the last provider request; not current occupancy. */
  measured_prompt_tokens?: number;
  projected_request_tokens?: number;
  last_request_prompt_tokens?: number;
  compaction_threshold?: number;
  surface_tokens?: number;
  tool_schema_tokens?: number;
  attachment_tokens?: number;
  used_ratio: number;
  reserved_output_tokens: number;
  reserved_tool_tokens: number;
  cache_hit_tokens: number;
  cache_miss_tokens: number;
  cache_hit_ratio?: number;
  compacted: boolean;
  blocks: ContextBlockUsage[];
}

/** Budget configuration (mirrors deepagent-app-core::BudgetConfig). */
export interface BudgetConfig {
  daily_limit: number | null;
  monthly_limit: number | null;
}

/** Accumulated cost summary (mirrors deepagent-app-core::CostSummary). */
/**
 * Session-wide cost and token statistics (billing ledger, cumulative).
 *
 * All token fields are CUMULATIVE across all requests in the session:
 * - `input_tokens`: sum of all `prompt_tokens` (billing dimension)
 * - `output_tokens`: sum of all `completion_tokens`
 *
 * This is the source for `SessionUsageBar` (bottom bar statistics).
 * For per-turn usage, see `TokenUsage`. For current window occupancy,
 * see `ContextUsageSnapshot.projected_request_tokens`.
 */
export interface CostSummary {
  session_cost: number;
  today_cost: number;
  month_cost: number;
  total_cost: number;
  currency: string;
  budget: BudgetConfig;
  /** Cumulative input tokens across ALL requests (billing ledger). */
  input_tokens: number;
  /** Cumulative output tokens across ALL requests (billing ledger). */
  output_tokens: number;
  /** Cumulative cache hit tokens across ALL requests. */
  cache_hit_tokens: number;
  /** Cumulative cache miss tokens across ALL requests. */
  cache_miss_tokens: number;
}

/** One per-currency balance row (mirrors deepagent-app-core::BalanceInfoDto). */
export interface BalanceInfo {
  currency: string;
  total_balance: string;
  granted_balance: string;
  topped_up_balance: string;
}

/** Account balance summary (mirrors deepagent-app-core::BalanceDto). */
export interface Balance {
  is_available: boolean;
  infos: BalanceInfo[];
}

export type DiagStatus = "ok" | "warning" | "error";

export interface DiagnosticResult {
  name: string;
  status: DiagStatus;
  detail: string;
  fix_hint: string | null;
}

/** File that was edited during a run (mirrors deepagent-runtime::EditedFile). */
export interface EditedFile {
  path: string;
  lines_added: number;
  lines_removed: number;
}

/** A message rendered in the chat view. */
export interface ChatMessage {
  id?: string;
  /** Transient presentation state; restored from the durable turn projection. */
  turn?: import("./components/chat-timeline/processDisclosure").TurnActivity;
  /** `system` rows are session-level markers (compaction boundary, injected
   * memories) that neither the user nor the model produced. */
  role: "user" | "assistant" | "system";
  content: string;
  /** Inline skill chips selected from slash/typeahead for this user turn. */
  selectedSkills?: ComposerSkillSelection[];
  /** Inline @ mentions selected as context for this user turn. */
  mentions?: ComposerMention[];
  /** Attachments displayed with a user turn. Model-readable attachment text is
   * kept out of this field and injected only into the backend prompt. */
  attachments?: ComposerAttachment[];
  tone?: "normal" | "error";
  /** Tool-call cards attached to an assistant turn (in invocation order). */
  tools?: ToolCall[];
  /** DeepSeek Thinking-Mode reasoning trace (shown as a collapsible block). */
  reasoning?: string;
  /**
   * Ordered parts of a live assistant turn (reasoning / tool / text) in the
   * exact order they streamed. When present, the view renders these in
   * sequence (preserving chronology); otherwise it falls back to the legacy
   * tools/reasoning/content fields (used by replayed timeline messages).
   */
  parts?: MessagePart[];
  /** Accumulated token usage for this turn (shown under the final answer). */
  usage?: TokenUsage;
  /** Wall-clock duration of the run that produced this turn, in ms. */
  runMs?: number;
  /** Files edited during this run (shown at turn completion). */
  editedFiles?: EditedFile[];
}

export type ComposerAttachmentKind = "text" | "image" | "file";
export type ComposerAttachmentSource = "paste" | "drop" | "picker";
export type ComposerAttachmentStatus = "ready" | "processing" | "error";

/**
 * Structured attachment reference sent with a chat run. Mirrors the Rust
 * `InputAttachment` tagged enum (`type` discriminator, snake_case fields).
 * Only the reference travels; image bytes are materialized into provider image
 * parts server-side at request time.
 */
export type RunAttachment =
  | { type: "image"; id: string; path: string; media_type?: string | null }
  | { type: "file"; id: string; path: string }
  | { type: "text"; id: string; content: string };

  backendMessage?: string;
  status: ComposerAttachmentStatus;
  error?: string;
}

  data_url?: string | null;
  text?: string | null;
}

/**
 * Opt-in advanced execution safeguards (§2.2/§2.3/§6.1/§6.2). All default OFF.
 * The matching `DEEPAGENT_*` env var force-enables each one regardless.
 */
export interface ExecutionFeatures {
  /** §2.3 stall/laziness detector: audit final answers for false completion. */
  stall_detector: boolean;
  /** §6.1 LLM command-injection guard on suspicious shell commands. */
  command_guard: boolean;
  /** §6.2 per-project trust gate: untrusted dirs escalate bash to approval. */
  project_trust: boolean;
  /** §2.2 adversarial goal verifier: skeptic panel audits goal coverage. */
  adversarial_verify: boolean;
}

  upstream: string | null;
  ahead: number;
  scanned_files: number;
  risks: GitPushRiskItem[];
  blocked_reason: string | null;
}

export interface GitCompareCommit {
  side: "base" | "target" | string;
  hash: string;
  full_hash: string;
  author_name: string;
  date: string;
  subject: string;
}

export interface GitRefCompare {
  project_path: string;
  repo_root: string | null;
  is_repo: boolean;
  base_ref: string;
  target_ref: string;
  merge_base: string | null;
  ahead: number;
  behind: number;
  commits: GitCompareCommit[];
  files: GitCommitFile[];
  blocked_reason: string | null;
}

export interface GitBatchCommitTarget {
  project_path: string;
  message: string | null;
}

export interface GitBatchCommitPreviewItem {
  project_path: string;
  repo_root: string | null;
  is_repo: boolean;
  current_branch: string | null;
  files_changed: number;
  staged_files: number;
  additions: number;
  deletions: number;
  ahead: number;
  behind: number;
  blocked_reason: string | null;
}

export interface GitBatchProjectResult {
  project_path: string;
  current_branch: string | null;
  ok: boolean;
  committed: boolean;
  pushed: boolean;
  skipped: boolean;
  message: string;
  commit_result: GitOperationResult | null;
  push_result: GitOperationResult | null;
}

export interface GitWorktree {
  path: string;
  head: string | null;
  branch: string | null;
  detached: boolean;
  bare: boolean;
}

/** §6.2 per-project workspace trust status. */

export interface ProjectMapStatus {

/** A knowledge entry (mirrors deepagent-app-core::KnowledgeDto). */

  updated_at: number;

// ===========================================================================
// Skill Marketplace (skillsmp.com + GitHub install flow)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Mirrors the Rust types reachable via the `skill_market_*` Tauri commands.
// Field-naming is verified against the actual `#[serde(...)]` attributes:
//   - MarketSkill / Pagination use field-level `rename = "camelCase"` for a
//     handful of keys (the rest stay snake_case).
//   - ScanReport / FileInfo / RiskItem stay snake_case (no crate-wide rename),
//     except `FileInfo` renames its Rust field `kind` to `type` on the wire.
//   - RiskCategory / RiskSeverity / SortBy / SkillsMpKeySource serialize as
//     lowercase string variants (`#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]`).
//   - MarketSearchInput is the only marketplace command struct with crate-wide
//     `rename_all = "camelCase"` — so its `sortBy` field is camelCase on the
//     wire.
// ===========================================================================

/** One row from `GET /api/v1/skills/search` (mirrors deepagent-skills::MarketSkill). */
export interface MarketSkill {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  author: string;
  description: string;
  /** GitHub source URL (`https://github.com/{owner}/{repo}/tree/{branch}/{path}`). */
  githubUrl: string;
  /** Canonical skillsmp.com page URL for the "view on web" link. */
  skillUrl: string;
  stars: number;
  /** Last update — unix epoch seconds. */
  updatedAt: number;
}

/** Pagination block from `data.pagination` (mirrors deepagent-skills::Pagination). */
export interface Pagination {
  page: number;
  limit: number;
  total: number;
  hasNext: boolean;
  hasPrev: boolean;
}

/** Search-response body envelope (mirrors deepagent-skills::MarketSearchData). */
export interface MarketSearchData {
  skills: MarketSkill[];
  pagination: Pagination;
}

/** Sort order. Lowercased on the wire. */
export type SortBy = "stars" | "recent";

/** Input to `skillMarketSearch`. Mirrors the desktop-side
 *  `MarketSearchInput` struct, which is `#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]`,
 *  so `sortBy` is camelCase on the wire. */
export interface MarketSearchInput {
  q?: string;
  page?: number;
  limit?: number;
  sortBy?: SortBy;
  category?: string;
  occupation?: string;
}

/** Coarse category of a static-scan finding. */
export type RiskCategory =
  | "shell"
  | "execution"
  | "network"
  | "credential"
  | "filesystem"
  | "exfiltration";

/** Severity tier of a static-scan finding. */
export type RiskSeverity = "safe" | "warning" | "danger";

/** One file entry (mirrors deepagent-skills::FileInfo).
 *  The Rust field `kind` is `#[serde(rename = "type")]` on the wire. */
export interface FileInfo {
  name: string;
  /** Lower-cased extension including the leading dot (e.g. `.py`), or `""`. */
  type: string;
  size: number;
}

/** One scan finding (mirrors deepagent-skills::RiskItem). */
export interface RiskItem {
  category: RiskCategory;
  severity: RiskSeverity;
  /** Forward-slashed path relative to the skill root. */
  file: string;
  /** 1-based line number; `null` for file-level rules. */
  line: number | null;
  detail: string;
}

/** Static-scan summary (mirrors deepagent-skills::ScanReport).
 *  All field names are snake_case to match the Rust struct (no rename). */
export interface ScanReport {
  name: string;
  skill_md_content: string;
  files: FileInfo[];
  risks: RiskItem[];
}

/** Result of `skill_market_scan` (mirrors the desktop-side `ScanResult`).
 *  Snake_case on the wire — no crate-wide rename. */
export interface ScanResult {
  temp_id: string;
  report: ScanReport;
}

/** AI security review verdict (mirrors deepagent-app-core::AiReviewResult).
 *  Snake_case on the wire. */
export interface AiReviewResult {
  passed: boolean;
  /** Full LLM output (analysis + verdict line). */
  raw_text: string;
  /** Captured one-line reason after `FAIL:`; `null` on PASS / parse error. */
  failure_reason: string | null;
}

/** Streaming-token event payload (Tauri event `skill-ai-review`). */
export interface SkillAiReviewToken {
  temp_id: string;
  token: string;
}

/** Final review event payload (Tauri event `skill-ai-review-done`).
 *  Exactly one of `result` / `error` is non-null. */
export interface SkillAiReviewDone {
  temp_id: string;
  result: AiReviewResult | null;
  error: string | null;
}

/** Provenance of the SkillsMP API key currently in use.
 *  Lowercased on the wire (mirrors deepagent-skills::ApiKeySource). */
export type SkillsMpKeySource = "user" | "builtin" | "none";

/** Result of `skill_market_get_api_key` (mirrors the desktop-side `ApiKeyInfo`).
 *  Never carries the key value itself. Snake_case on the wire. */
export interface ApiKeyInfo {
  has_user_key: boolean;
  source: SkillsMpKeySource;
}

/** Result of `skill_market_test_key` (mirrors the desktop-side `TestKeyResult`).
 *  Snake_case on the wire. */
export interface TestKeyResult {
  ok: boolean;
  /** `X-RateLimit-Daily-Remaining` from the most recent response, or `null`. */
  daily_remaining: number | null;
  /** Error message when `ok === false`; `null` on success. */
  error: string | null;
}

/** Tool-call result shape disclosed by the `skill` tool (channel B).
 *  Mirrors deepagent-skills::SkillToolOutput. `base_dir` is omitted on the
 *  wire when the skill carries no on-disk root. */
export interface SkillToolOutput {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  body: string;
  base_dir?: string;
  resources: string[];
}

/*
 * Translucent sidebar: when enabled on <html>, the window uses Tauri's Mica
 * effect and sidebar surfaces render at reduced opacity so the OS material
 * shows through. Body/root are transparent only in that mode; the main
 * content column keeps its own opaque background (bg-white / bg-bg-base),
 * so only the sidebar band actually reveals Mica.
 */
html.theme-translucent-sidebar,
html.theme-translucent-sidebar body,
html.theme-translucent-sidebar #root {
  background-color: transparent !important;
}

html.theme-translucent-sidebar .bg-sidebar-bg {
  background-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--theme-sidebar) 55%, transparent) !important;
  backdrop-filter: saturate(180%) blur(18px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: saturate(180%) blur(18px);
}
html.theme-translucent-sidebar.dark .bg-sidebar-bg {
  background-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--theme-sidebar) 65%, transparent) !important;
}

/* 入场动画只配 open 态：关闭时必须显式清掉 animation，否则 Radix Presence
   检测到 animationName 非 none 会一直等 animationend（已播完不再触发），
   面板永不卸载并持续占据焦点（行内输入框 autoFocus 被吞的根因）。 */
.popover-menu[data-state="closed"],
.floating-menu-panel[data-state="closed"] {
  animation: none;
  will-change: auto;
}

.floating-menu-item {
  animation: floating-menu-item-in 220ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1) 60ms both;
}

/* ===== theme/themePresets.ts · SHA256 9fe013e86b0502c94158b3b245a15c2f42f37e52247aafb920668ca4c6a9d46b ===== */
import type { ThemePalette, ThemePreset, ThemeVariant } from "./themeTypes";

function palette(p: ThemePalette): ThemePalette {
  return Object.freeze({ ...p });
}

function preset(id: string, name: string, variants: Partial<Record<ThemeVariant, ThemePalette>>): ThemePreset {
  return Object.freeze({
    id,
    name,
    source: "builtin",
    variants: Object.freeze({ ...variants }) as Partial<Record<ThemeVariant, ThemePalette>>,
  }) as ThemePreset;
}

export const BUILTIN_PRESETS: readonly ThemePreset[] = Object.freeze([
  preset("codex", "Codex", { light: CODEX_LIGHT, dark: CODEX_DARK }),
  preset("absolutely", "Absolutely", { light: ABSOLUTELY_LIGHT, dark: ABSOLUTELY_DARK }),
  preset("ayu", "Ayu", { light: AYU_LIGHT, dark: AYU_DARK }),
  preset("catppuccin", "Catppuccin", { light: CATPPUCCIN_LIGHT, dark: CATPPUCCIN_DARK }),
  preset("dracula", "Dracula", { dark: DRACULA_DARK }),
  preset("everforest", "Everforest", { light: EVERFOREST_LIGHT, dark: EVERFOREST_DARK }),
  preset("github", "GitHub", { light: GITHUB_LIGHT, dark: GITHUB_DARK }),
  preset("gruvbox", "Gruvbox", { light: GRUVBOX_LIGHT, dark: GRUVBOX_DARK }),
  preset("linear", "Linear", { light: LINEAR_LIGHT, dark: LINEAR_DARK }),
  preset("notion", "Notion", { light: NOTION_LIGHT, dark: NOTION_DARK }),
  preset("one", "One", { light: ONE_LIGHT, dark: ONE_DARK }),
]);

export function getPresetById(id: string): ThemePreset | undefined {
  return BUILTIN_PRESETS.find((p) => p.id === id);
}

export const DEFAULT_DARK_PRESET_ID = "codex";


/* ===== theme/themeTokens.ts · SHA256 84f12a635a81715c0f3283d0e3de02f0287cd28762b507378caa45627976d752 ===== */
import { mixHex } from "./colorUtils";
import type { ThemePalette } from "./themeTypes";

// The contrast slider (0-100) reshapes derived tokens around a neutral of 50.
// Below 50 we pull derived colors toward the background (softer separation).
// Above 50 we push them toward the foreground (crisper separation).
function contrastBlend(
  base: string,
  towardFg: string,
  towardBg: string,
  contrast: number,
  fgWeight: number,
  bgWeight: number,
): string {
  const c = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, contrast));
  const delta = (c - 50) / 50;
  if (delta === 0) return base;
  if (delta > 0) {
    return mixHex(base, towardFg, delta * fgWeight);
  }
  return mixHex(base, towardBg, -delta * bgWeight);
}

export function paletteToTokens(palette: ThemePalette): Record<string, string> {
  const { background, foreground, contrast } = palette;
  const foregroundMuted = contrastBlend(
    palette.foregroundMuted,
    foreground,
    background,
    contrast,
    0.45,
    0.35,
  );
  const border = contrastBlend(
    palette.border,
    foreground,
    background,
    contrast,
    0.35,
    0.45,
  );
  const hover = contrastBlend(
    palette.hover,
    foreground,
    background,
    contrast,
    0.25,
    0.45,
  );
  const selection = contrastBlend(
    palette.selection,
    palette.accent,
    background,
    contrast,
    0.2,
    0.35,
  );
  const sidebar = contrastBlend(
    palette.sidebar,
    foreground,
    background,
    contrast,
    0.12,
    0.35,
  );
  return {
    "--theme-accent": palette.accent,
    "--theme-accent-hover": palette.accentHover,
    "--theme-bg": palette.background,
    "--theme-sidebar": sidebar,
    "--theme-elevated": palette.elevated,
    "--theme-fg": palette.foreground,
    "--theme-text-secondary": foregroundMuted,
    "--theme-border": border,
    "--theme-hover": hover,
    "--theme-selection": selection,
  };
}

export function applyTokens(
  tokens: Record<string, string>,
  fontTokens: { uiFont: string; codeFont: string },
  isDark: boolean
): void {
  const root = document.documentElement;
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(tokens)) {
    root.style.setProperty(k, v);
  }
  root.style.setProperty("--ui-font", fontTokens.uiFont);
  root.style.setProperty("--code-font", fontTokens.codeFont);
  if (isDark) {
    root.classList.add("dark");
  } else {
    root.classList.remove("dark");
  }
}


/* ===== theme/themeTypes.ts · SHA256 09984375dd953369cdaee7e28d7cdbb70be0e59ec8650adf08393aecaa79db12 ===== */
export type ThemeMode = "light" | "dark" | "system";

export interface ThemeSwitchOrigin {
  x: number;
  y: number;
}

  accent: string;
  accentHover: string;
  background: string;
  sidebar: string;
  elevated: string;
  foreground: string;
  foregroundMuted: string;
  border: string;
  hover: string;
  selection: string;
  contrast: number;
}

export interface ThemePreset {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  source: ThemePresetSource;
  variants: Partial<Record<ThemeVariant, ThemePalette>>;
  createdAt?: string;
  updatedAt?: string;
}

export interface ThemePreferences {
  uiFont: string;
  codeFont: string;
  translucentSidebar: boolean;
}

  schemaVersion: 2;
  mode: ThemeMode;
  selectedPresetIds: Record<ThemeVariant, string>;
  workingPalettes: Record<ThemeVariant, ThemePalette>;
  preferences: ThemePreferences;
  customPresets: ThemePreset[];
  revision: number;
}

export interface ThemeImportResult {
  ok: boolean;
  presetId?: string;
  variant?: ThemeVariant;
  error?: string;
}

/* ===== theme/colorUtils.ts · SHA256 7d6c3b9b0ff276ba621f37c7c45c2cf3a4e8fc0b2e5f0a9647276c5cc733e770 ===== */
export interface Rgb {
  r: number;
  g: number;
  b: number;
}

export function parseHex(hex: string): Rgb | null {
  if (typeof hex !== "string") return null;
  const m = hex.trim().match(/^#([0-9A-Fa-f]{3}|[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}|[0-9A-Fa-f]{8})$/);
  if (!m) return null;
  let h = m[1];
  if (h.length === 3) {
    h = h[0] + h[0] + h[1] + h[1] + h[2] + h[2];
  }
  return {
    r: parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16),
    g: parseInt(h.slice(2, 4), 16),
    b: parseInt(h.slice(4, 6), 16),
  };
}

export function formatHex({ r, g, b }: Rgb): string {
  const c = (v: number) =>
    Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(v))).toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
  return `#${c(r)}${c(g)}${c(b)}`.toUpperCase();
}

export function mixHex(a: string, b: string, t: number): string {
  const ca = parseHex(a);
  const cb = parseHex(b);
  if (!ca || !cb) return a;
  const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
  return formatHex({
    r: ca.r + (cb.r - ca.r) * k,
    g: ca.g + (cb.g - ca.g) * k,
    b: ca.b + (cb.b - ca.b) * k,
  });
}

function channelLuminance(v: number): number {
  const s = v / 255;
  return s <= 0.03928 ? s / 12.92 : Math.pow((s + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
}

export function relativeLuminance(rgb: Rgb): number {
  return (
    0.2126 * channelLuminance(rgb.r) +
    0.7152 * channelLuminance(rgb.g) +
    0.0722 * channelLuminance(rgb.b)
  );
}

export function contrastRatio(hexA: string, hexB: string): number {
  const a = parseHex(hexA);
  const b = parseHex(hexB);
  if (!a || !b) return 21;
  const la = relativeLuminance(a);
  const lb = relativeLuminance(b);
  const lighter = Math.max(la, lb);
  const darker = Math.min(la, lb);
  return (lighter + 0.05) / (darker + 0.05);
}
